# 工具契约参考

<!-- Public projection of the source tool reference, produced by export-editions.mjs. -->

本页随源码快照提供；公共工具来源与完整结构见下文。版本和验证状态见 [EDITION.md](../EDITION.md)。

本页从当前源码的实际注册表、MCP tools/list 与 Tutor 状态投影生成。完整输入／输出 JSON Schema、示例、路由卡和预算策略保存在同次生成的 [tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)；每项下方给出 JSON Pointer，嵌套约束以该完整结构为准。

维护共同 Book 参数／示例时编辑 `book-tool-contracts`；状态示例编辑 `tutor::spec/spec_for_context`；其他工具按各项来源符号修改。工具清单从原所有者枚举，新工具进入原目录后由生成命令收录。

## 表面与能力边界

| 表面 | 工具数 | 边界 |
|---|---:|---|
| [Resident](#resident) | 56 | 完整注册表；每轮实际可见集合由工具暴露计划按权限、证据、内容 profile 和激活状态选择。Tutor 另按当前状态投影。 |
| [本地 MCP](#local_mcp) | 17 | 本地工作区发布的 Book 与 Artifact 工具；Book query、synthesize 和 visitor guide 可调用模型。 |
| [专用 Build Executor](#executor) | 4 | 仅专用 understand_book_executor 使用。按 handoff → 输入批次 → GENERATE → 提交候选推进，引用与序号取当次工具返回值。 |

Resident 结果 schema 描述 ToolResultEnvelope，Provider 函数 parameters 仍仅描述输入。ok／partial／error、预算省略与采样后回收的 null 按实际交付解释。定位候选与结构投影只提供位置；正文证据以实际读取和接纳范围为准。

本地 MCP 的 outputSchema 描述成功的 structuredContent；isError=true 的错误沿独立诊断协议处理。

Executor 在 MCP text content 中交付版本化 session 响应，依 action.kind 推进；示例引用来自合成测试 session，使用时替换为当次返回值。提交候选的接纳与终态由代码回执决定。

## 任务入口

- 查找字面出现：从 [Resident 搜索示例](#resident-book-search_text-examples) 选定范围，再按结果读取正文。
- 继续教学：先核对 [Tutor 状态](#tutor-states)，使用该状态实际发布的示例与引用。
- 修改演示：沿 [Presentation 示例](#resident-presentation-author-examples) 读取既有对象、修改候选、预览并交付。
- 保存读者要求的笔记：使用 [reader.note 示例](#resident-reader-note-examples)，依据实际保存回执判断成果。
- 推进构建：从 [executor.open 示例](#executor-executor-open-examples) 开始，按工具返回的 next_request 和 GENERATE 继续。

<a id="resident-agent-progress"></a>

### agent.progress

Share one to three brief user-facing sentences about the next action, actual completed work, or a changed approach. Call alongside actual work in the same batch. No private reasoning, arguments, internal IDs, source markers, teaching content or unreviewed answers. This update supplies no evidence or delivery.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/0`。

**适用**：Briefly explain the next action, meaningful completed work or a changed approach alongside actual work.

**避免**：Do not expose private reasoning, internal locators, source markers, teaching content or unreviewed answers; updates are not evidence or delivery.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryReceipt`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `message` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1 | Brief public progress update in the user's language. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-artifact-list"></a>

### artifact.list

List bounded Routing Cards for the turn-frozen current active accepted artifact overlay. Routing Cards are discovery metadata, not book evidence.

**来源符号**：[crates/artifact-tools/src/lib.rs:artifact_list_input_schema/artifact_search_input_schema/artifact_read_input_schema](../crates/artifact-tools/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/1`。

**适用**：Inspect bounded routing metadata for the active accepted artifact overlay.

**避免**：Do not treat Routing Cards as book evidence or call without an active overlay.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`active_artifact_overlay`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ArtifactProjection`，model_body 上限 12288 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cursor` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | — |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=1; maximum=50 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-artifact-read"></a>

### artifact.read

Read at most three bounded records from a turn-frozen artifact using opaque refs returned by artifact.search or a read continuation. Artifact data is not canonical Book evidence.

**来源符号**：[crates/artifact-tools/src/lib.rs:artifact_list_input_schema/artifact_search_input_schema/artifact_read_input_schema](../crates/artifact-tools/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/2`。

**适用**：Read bounded artifact records returned by artifact.search or its continuation.

**避免**：Do not invent opaque refs or treat artifact records as canonical book evidence.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`active_artifact_overlay`、`artifact_search_hit`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ArtifactProjection`，model_body 上限 12288 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `artifact_ref` | 是 | type="string" | — |
| `cursor` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Read continuation; omit record_refs when using cursor. |
| `field_paths` | 按分支／可选 | type=["array","null"]; minItems=1; maxItems=32 | — |
| `include_relations` | 按分支／可选 | type="boolean"; default=false | — |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=1; maximum=3 | — |
| `record_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type=["array","null"]; minItems=1; maxItems=3 | Opaque refs returned by artifact.search; mutually exclusive with cursor. Their count must not exceed limit. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-artifact-search"></a>

### artifact.search

Search the turn-frozen current active accepted artifacts with deterministic bounded lexical ranking. Call at most once initially per user turn; a zero-hit result must not be retried with rewritten guesses.

**来源符号**：[crates/artifact-tools/src/lib.rs:artifact_list_input_schema/artifact_search_input_schema/artifact_read_input_schema](../crates/artifact-tools/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/3`。

**适用**：Search a relevant active accepted artifact after its Routing Card matches the task.

**避免**：Do not retry rewritten guesses after zero hits or use artifact data as canonical book evidence.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`active_artifact_overlay`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ArtifactProjection`，model_body 上限 12288 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anchor_lids` | 按分支／可选 | type=["array","null"]; minItems=1; maxItems=64 | — |
| `artifact_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type=["array","null"]; minItems=1; maxItems=50 | — |
| `cursor` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | — |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=1; maximum=3 | — |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=512 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-board-author"></a>

### board.author

Use this accepted board when a visual relationship helps the current request. A short explanation, correction or clarification can be answered directly without authoring a diagram. Prioritize the host-selected explanation block over the rest of a long previous answer. When drawing is useful, read the relevant board layout and exact sources. Call plan with the concrete relationship before preparing new visuals or changing the explanation strategy; consume its coach handoff in the next sampling before drawing. Plan is run-local and reused by later prepare/revise/preview calls. Layout, font or position-only adjustments do not need a new plan. Search catalog or workspace.records.read only when relevant existing material or original conversations are needed. catalog searches all permitted sources before returning a bounded page; source_read returns an exact original snapshot and prepare imports resolves that same source identity. Workspace notes use kind note and stable note_id; their current text and shared relations are resolved from workspace.notes. Use workspace.notes relation_save to connect two note cards with a label and reason, never a duplicate native board link. Projected note_relation_id links are read-only and cannot be route/mapping targets. State the current learning workspace scope and never fabricate history or understanding. Choose the visual structure that explains the requested relationship. Use native drawings with text, shapes, paths and labelled links for flowcharts, causal networks, comparisons, spatial groupings and other freeform diagrams; circle/highlight with paths; connect explanations to visuals with mappings and progressive focus/reveal routes. Update existing native drawings through update_drawings and retained geometry through layout while keeping original answers and learner quotes immutable. Read the accepted persistent board, prepare native drawings with stable explanation blocks and a local teaching route, inspect the real Reader BoardCanvas preview, then stage exactly that observed candidate for the final successful answer. read returns the reserved answer item ID, current content revision and exact retained knowledge/understanding snapshots. Each prepare replaces the candidate and invalidates its preview. New content references use base_board_revision+1; existing references retain their exact observed revision. Every explanation block has an explicit stable block_id; never infer blocks from Markdown positions. Cite new source claims only with this turn's observed source.present references. Drawing based_on references identify formal conditions or actual learner evidence; they do not establish mastery. Do not fabricate learner quotes or understanding records; use tutor.step evidence for real user expressions. Preview images must be inspected in a later model sampling before deliver. Use commit to publish one complete observed part and keep working. A committed part survives cancellation or failure; read returns its exact receipts and the next reserved answer item ID. After any parts are committed, explicitly commit every additional part and finish with references to the saved parts. Deliver keeps the whole-turn staging path only when no parts have been committed. If the board changes, read and prepare/preview again. Existing presentations use declared real anchor IDs and exact PresentationRef; this tool never changes their experimental state. Board source citations: Substantive claims about the book, including its rules, conditions and specific values, require clickable original-source citations in direct text answers, explanation blocks and each committed part. Read the relevant book/library evidence in this turn and call source.present with the supporting observed passage. Then place [[source:<source_ref_id>]] after the supported sentence or closely related group of claims, using the exact returned ID; calling source.present alone does not attach a citation. Put these markers in blocks[].markdown before prepare/revise, so the saved explanation blocks and committed parts retain clickable sources. A derivation must identify its cited source premises and distinguish the inference from the author's statement. Label learner assumptions and illustrative values as such; they are not book evidence. Historical source_ref_ids and retained source_bindings are not this turn's evidence: reacquire support for new book claims, and cite only the references actually supporting the current explanation. If a required claim has no supporting observed passage, state the gap or narrow the claim; never invent a citation. Greetings, interface operations and layout-only changes need no forced citation. A concise recap may reuse clear nearby citations without repeating them. Compose around a clear visual model using shapes, spatial groups and labelled links. Give related nodes consistent alignment, generous whitespace and a small muted color palette. Keep node headings short and supporting text sparse; allocate enough space for ordinary headings around 22-26 px and body text around 16-19 px at natural scale. Put long explanations in blocks instead of shrinking text to cram the whole board onto one screen. Use local route focus to make each group readable while preserving the full model's relationships. Inspect hierarchy, spacing and readable text in the real preview. Continue revising only to fix a concrete wrong relationship, overlap, unreadable text or missing requested meaning; once those issues are resolved, deliver the inspected candidate. revise changes only the supplied existing blocks by block_id and drawings by item_id in the current uncommitted candidate, preserving every other item, link, mapping and route. Supply at least one change. It creates a new revision and invalidates the previous preview and teaching approval. Read collection=candidate_blocks for the original source-bound blocks. Preview and inspect the revised candidate before selecting it by move.board_candidate_revision; never rewrite its teaching body at select. For an explicitly requested mind map or a genuinely hierarchical outline, use operations create_mind_map and insert_topic with short topic titles and based_on references. Only these topics have derived tree edges and geometry. Freeform diagrams use drawings, links and explicit positions; drawings are full diagram content, not limited to annotations. Choose the form from the user request and the relationship being explained, not from whether a mind map already exists on the board. Both forms can coexist. Preserve the existing map and unrelated items when adding a different diagram. Reorganizing old drawings into a new mind map still requires the existing conversion confirmation. A selected topic scopes edits to that topic and descendants. Explanation or clarification requests may be answered as text only; expand, add an example, simplify or reorganize uses semantic operations without replacing unrelated branches. Use stable IDs and put_link/put_mapping/put_route to update existing relationships rather than duplicate them. On an unrelated human edit, read or preview rebases the existing candidate, changes its candidate_revision, and clears earlier preview and teaching approval. Inspect the new actual preview and repeat active teaching selection. On candidate_pending_changes, preserve the human result and offer to redo from current content; do not recreate deleted targets or overwrite the conflicting branch. Ordinary topic layout accepts no model coordinates except a new map's root anchor.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/4`。

**适用**：Search relevant original workspace sources and conversations, collect exact snapshots, build and edit freeform diagrams, flowcharts, comparisons or editable mind maps on the same board, and explain through mappings and reveal/focus routes; preview before committing.

**避免**：Available for an accepted whole-board request or exact item focus, including blank boards. Keep retained answers and learner quotations immutable; edit mind-map topics through atomic operations and update free drawings or retained layout explicitly. A selected topic limits changes to its subtree. Never invent history, understanding or mastery; inspect the real candidate before publishing.

**效果与前提**：effect=`presentation_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`high`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `after` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | — |
| `base_board_revision` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | — |
| `blocks` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | The explanation needed for this request and this visual relationship. Attach this turn's returned [[source:&lt;source_ref_id&gt;]] markers to substantive book claims in markdown; source.present calls without markers do not create clickable citations. Explain how to read the new visual or resolve the selected point; do not repeat the previous full answer merely to attach a diagram. Keep prose here and map it to concise visual nodes. |
| `board_revision` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | Pin continuation pages to the observed board revision; read again after a conflict. |
| `candidate_revision` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | — |
| `collection` | 按分支／可选 | enum=["committed","layout","items","links","mappings","routes","mind_maps","understanding_items","understanding_refs","formal_condition_refs","candidate_items","candidate_blocks","candidate_updates","candidate_links","candidate_mappings","candidate_routes","candidate_operations"] | Read complete records from one collection. layout returns every item's identity and geometry without long retained answer text. Follow next_calls to recover omitted collections. |
| `drawings` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | The concrete relationship the reader should understand, especially the selected explanation's unresolved point. Call plan before designing new visuals or changing the explanation strategy. |
| `imports` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Resolve exact original sources into the private candidate; full content and provenance are server owned. |
| `kind` | 按分支／可选 | enum=["personal_note","note","answer","presentation","prebuild","understanding"] | — |
| `layout` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Move or resize retained items or this prepare's answer/drawings without changing content. To move the new explanation card away from a topic map after preview, use read.reserved_answer_item_id here. Topic geometry is derived; move its map with set_mind_map instead. Use aligned groups and generous gaps so relationships and labels stay readable. |
| `length` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1; maximum=50 | — |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1; maximum=50 | — |
| `links` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `mappings` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `needs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Unresolved evidence or representation needs for this explanation, not drawing coordinates. |
| `offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | — |
| `operation` | 是 | enum=["read","catalog","source_read","plan","prepare","revise","preview","deliver","commit"] | — |
| `operations` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Semantic mind-map edits, using the same atomic operations as the manual editor. Prepare: ordered operation batch against base_board_revision. Revise: replace the entire uncommitted batch while preserving other candidate content. Parent order is children index; never draw tree edges manually or guess topic coordinates. Insert parent before children. Stable IDs survive rebase and undo. |
| `presentations` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Exact presentation versions already delivered by presentation.author in this run. They become independent retained board items in this part; do not invent Markdown presentation markers. |
| `published_book_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `query` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Search the full permitted source contents before pagination, including answers beyond the first page. |
| `read_item_ids` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Exact retained item IDs whose current content this edit depends on. Include shared notes read for this explanation. The host checks these plus all writes before rebasing; do not list unrelated branches. |
| `routes` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Focus each teaching step on a small related group and its connecting links. Keep the overall relationship model coherent instead of fitting all details into every step. |
| `source` | 按分支／可选 | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false | Copy the exact source object from catalog. Historical answers and presentations resolve their original publication automatically. |
| `update_drawings` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Explicitly replace native graphic/text/geometry of existing drawings using their exact item_id, including visual refinements to an existing board. Preserve source meaning and provenance; prior revisions stay readable. |
| `viewport` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-concept"></a>

### book.concept

Deterministically recall whole-book concept and entity candidates with match reasons, bounded previews, and complete occurrence LIDs. Video graph candidates also retain distinct object meanings, text/visual/joint support, relations and video.read_frames hints. Read chosen text with book.text and actual video frames before citing.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/5`。

**适用**：Discover concept or entity candidates when the user may not know the graph display label.

**避免**：Do not treat previews or graph support as observed evidence, or silently select a homonym. Read chosen occurrence LIDs with book.text and video_graph.read_hints with video.read_frames; pure visual objects have no invented LIDs.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`medium`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anchor_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID in this book to rank nearby candidates and previews. Omit or null for unanchored ranking; this does not restrict recall to a subtree. |
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | anyOf: type="string"; enum=["definition","procedure","explanation","example"] / type="null" | Optional kind of source passage to locate for this concept. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; default=12; minimum=1; maximum=50 | Maximum concept/entity candidates, 1..50; omit or null for 12. Inspect matched_count and truncated for candidate coverage; this does not limit occurrences within a candidate. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | Nonblank concept/entity name, alias or descriptive terms for deterministic candidate recall from names and occurrence text. At most 4,096 Unicode scalar values, including surrounding whitespace; not literal occurrence enumeration. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-concept-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "anchor_lid": "1.2",
    "limit": 5,
    "query": "threshold"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `model_body` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=true / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / 任意 JSON 值 |
| `model_body.matched_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Graph candidates matched by this query's recall rules before limit, not all semantic matches in the book or proof that graph construction is complete. |
| `model_body.returned_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Candidates selected by limit before transport pagination or model-output budgeting. |
| `model_body.truncated` | type="boolean" — Whether limit omitted matched candidates; does not describe occurrence coverage. |
| `status` | const="ok" / const="partial" / const="error" / type="string"; enum=["ok","partial","error"] |
| `truncated` | const=false / type="boolean" |
| `model_body.delivery.status` | const="partial" / const="omitted" |
| `continuation` | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt.status` | type="string"; enum=["ok","error","legacy_unparsed"] |
| `version` | const="tool_result_envelope.v1" |

<a id="resident-book-context"></a>

### book.context

Return context pointers for a LID: near adds direct mathematical relationships in both directions, tree adjacency and local edges; mid adds other concept or entity occurrences; far adds long-range edges. Mathematical links preserve uses, proves and asks_to_prove, with next_calls covering the related statement's evidence. Counts cover only known rule-recalled pointers, not all semantic relations; built structure availability is reported by book.structure. Pointers contain no source text; use book.text to read it.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/6`。

**适用**：Expand around a verified LID through deterministic context links.

**避免**：Do not treat pointers as source text.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `granularity` | 按分支／可选 | default="near"; anyOf: type="string"; enum=["near","mid","far"] / type="null" | Context reach: near includes tree adjacency and local relations; mid adds other concept/entity occurrences; far also adds long-range relations. Omit or null for near. |
| `k` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; default=10; minimum=0 | Maximum number of context pointers returned across all layers. Omit or null for 10; zero returns no items. total_candidates counts deduplicated pointers before k; returned_count and truncated describe selection by k before pagination or output budgeting. Counts pointers, not distinct LIDs, characters or source passages. |
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | Known LID in this book to expand around. Returned items are context pointers; read their LIDs through the source-text tool for evidence. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-context-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "granularity": "near",
    "k": 3,
    "lid": "1.2"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `model_body` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=true / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / 任意 JSON 值 |
| `model_body.returned_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Pointers selected by k in the canonical result, before transport pagination or the Resident model-output budget is applied. |
| `model_body.truncated` | type="boolean" — Whether k omitted known candidates; false does not imply semantic completeness or availability of built structure artifacts. |
| `status` | const="ok" / const="partial" / const="error" / type="string"; enum=["ok","partial","error"] |
| `truncated` | const=false / type="boolean" |
| `model_body.delivery.status` | const="partial" / const="omitted" |
| `continuation` | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt.status` | type="string"; enum=["ok","error","legacy_unparsed"] |
| `version` | const="tool_result_envelope.v1" |

<a id="resident-book-guide_path"></a>

### book.guide_path

Return a macro guided-reading route from BookStructure by expanding key stops along spine segments. It does not interpret natural language or read Reader or memory state; when the sidecar is absent, it returns explicit unavailable status.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/7`。

**适用**：Choose macro reading stops from BookStructure.

**避免**：Do not use for semantic question answering.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `at` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID in this book to locate its structural role or current route segment. Omit or null for the whole-book view; this is a location, not a natural-language query. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-guided_route_from"></a>

### book.guided_route_from

Return a teaching-shaped navigation frontier from a LID: route_from plus technical_learning ordering, with the five groups reordered by teaching priority and empty groups removed. Returns ordered [{category, steps}] groups. Prefer this zero-LLM tool for resident guided reading; raw route_from is for lower-level or visitor use. All LIDs and edges are real.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/8`。

**适用**：Inspect a teaching-ordered navigation frontier from a verified LID.

**避免**：Do not use outside guided reading or treat navigation edges as source evidence.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `at` | 是 | type="string" | Starting LID |
| `k` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer" | Optional top-K frontier size per group |

<a id="resident-book-paper_metadata"></a>

### book.paper_metadata

Return the current paper's metadata projection while preserving value, source, evidence_lids, and confidence. It returns explicit unavailable status without a sidecar and never creates cross-paper relationships.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/10`。

**适用**：Read the current paper metadata projection.

**避免**：Do not infer cross-paper relations.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`paper_content_profile`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | 无具名参数 | 见完整 schema |

<a id="resident-book-paper_reading_guide"></a>

### book.paper_reading_guide

Return the read-only PaperReadingGuide projection, combining paper metadata and lexicon, BookStructure, graph, discourse, and source text into ten paper questions, a codebook, and abstract-reading aids. It never creates or changes persistent truth.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/11`。

**适用**：Read a deterministic paper-reading projection.

**避免**：Do not use it to mutate persistent truth.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`paper_content_profile`；relative_cost=`medium`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

| `stage` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["passive","active","critical","creative"]; default="passive" | Reading stage: passive, active, critical or creative. Defaults to passive. |

<a id="resident-book-query"></a>

### book.query

Answer a self-contained semantic question about explicit referents by resolving targets first and then reading source evidence around the frozen bindings.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/12`。

**适用**：Answer definition, explanation, relation, or comparison obligations for explicit referents.

**避免**：Do not use for literal occurrence location.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`high`。

**交付策略**：`QueryResponse`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": false,
  "openWorldHint": true,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anchor_lid` | 是 | type="string" | Required known LID in this book anchoring referent resolution. It provides local context without restricting evidence retrieval to this node. |
| `intent` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["definition","explanation","relation","comparison"] | Question kind: definition or explanation requires 1..3 targets; relation or comparison requires 2..3 separately named targets. |
| `obligations` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=3 | One to three answer requirements, each in its own requirement object; these guide evidence sufficiency for the requested answer. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string" | Self-contained, nonblank semantic question about the named targets, with enough context to resolve referents. Use literal search for exact wording or first/previous occurrences. |
| `targets` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=3 | Explicit referents to resolve before reading evidence. Each item is nonblank and unique after trimming and lowercasing; name comparison/relation targets separately, not as one combined phrase. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
[
  {
    "properties": {
      "intent": {
        "enum": [
          "definition",
          "explanation"
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  {
    "properties": {
      "intent": {
        "enum": [
          "relation",
          "comparison"
        ]
      },
      "targets": {
        "minItems": 2
      }
    }
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-book-query-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "anchor_lid": "1.1",
    "intent": "comparison",
    "obligations": [
      {
        "requirement": "State the activation condition at each location."
      },
      {
        "requirement": "Compare whether equality activates each threshold, citing both passages."
      }
    ],
    "query": "How do the activation conditions of the alpha threshold and beta threshold differ?",
    "targets": [
      "alpha threshold",
      "beta threshold"
    ]
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `model_body` | anyOf: oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=true / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / 任意 JSON 值 |
| `model_body.status` | type="string"; enum=["complete"] / type="string"; enum=["partial"] / type="string"; enum=["insufficient"] / type="string"; enum=["invalid_plan"] / type="string"; enum=["ambiguous"] / type="string"; enum=["unresolved"] |
| `status` | const="ok" / const="partial" / const="error" / type="string"; enum=["ok","partial","error"] |
| `truncated` | const=false / type="boolean" |
| `model_body.delivery.status` | const="partial" / const="omitted" |
| `continuation` | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false |

<a id="resident-book-route_from"></a>

### book.route_from

Return a deterministic navigation frontier from a LID in five semantic groups: back prerequisites, forward depth, concretize examples, cross relationships, and continue reading order. Every step contains a real LID and real edge. This zero-LLM tool decides where to go next.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/13`。

**适用**：Inspect deterministic navigation choices from a verified LID.

**避免**：Do not use navigation edges as source evidence.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `at` | 是 | type="string" | Starting LID |
| `k` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer" | Optional top-K frontier size per group |

<a id="resident-book-route_from-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

当前定义未发布输入示例。

**结果与覆盖**

当前定义未发布 outputSchema；结果／效果按以上工具说明、路由元数据和原执行回执解释。

<a id="resident-book-route_to"></a>

### book.route_to

Find a deterministic from-to path in the navigation graph using BFS, returning navigation steps with only real LIDs and edges. target must already be a resolved LID located through book.concept or book.context.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/14`。

**适用**：Find a deterministic route between two already resolved LIDs.

**避免**：Do not use unresolved targets or treat the route as source evidence.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `from` | 是 | type="string" | Starting LID |
| `k` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer" | Optional hop budget |
| `target` | 是 | type="string" | Resolved target LID |

<a id="resident-book-route_to-examples"></a>

<a id="resident-book-search"></a>

### book.search

Find relevant passages in the current material using lightweight lexical relevance ranking over canonical source. No graph, embeddings or semantic prebuild is required. Results are bounded candidates, not evidence or exhaustive semantic matches.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/15`。

**适用**：Locate passages for a natural-language question when the source location is unknown; optionally restrict to a known subtree. Read each chosen passage with its returned book.text call before explaining or citing.

**避免**：For an already selected passage read it directly. For first, previous or every exact occurrence use book.search_text.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | anyOf: type="string"; enum=["definition","procedure","explanation","example"] / type="null" | Optional kind of original passage to locate using the book's concept and discourse index. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; default=8; minimum=1; maximum=20 | Maximum ranked candidate passages, 1..20; omit or null for 8. Scores rank candidates and are not confidence. A bounded result cannot establish semantic exhaustiveness. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | Nonblank natural-language question or terms to find relevant canonical-source passages. Ranking is lexical; translated or synonymous terms can help when the original wording differs. |
| `within_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID restricting candidates to this node and its descendants. Omit or null to search the current material. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-search-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "limit": 4,
    "query": "threshold activation conditions",
    "within_lid": "1"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `model_body` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=true / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / 任意 JSON 值 |
| `model_body.delivery.returned_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 |
| `model_body.delivery.status` | enum=["complete","partial"] / const="omitted" / const="partial" |
| `model_body.next_calls` | type="array" |
| `status` | const="ok" / const="partial" / const="error" / type="string"; enum=["ok","partial","error"] |
| `truncated` | const=false / type="boolean" |
| `continuation` | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt.status` | type="string"; enum=["ok","error","legacy_unparsed"] |
| `version` | const="tool_result_envelope.v1" |

<a id="resident-book-search_text"></a>

### book.search_text

Enumerate the complete set of literal-text occurrences in canonical source, returning stable source UTF-16 ranges, the total count, and a pagination cursor.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/16`。

**适用**：Locate the first, previous, or every lexical occurrence before interpreting it.

**避免**：Do not use lexical exhaustiveness as proof of semantic exhaustiveness.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cursor` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Opaque next_cursor from the preceding search result. Omit or null for the first page; preserve query, match_mode, scope, order and page_size on continuation. A changed source revision invalidates it. |
| `match_mode` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["exact","normalized"]; default="exact" | exact (default) matches unchanged text; normalized applies NFKC, full non-Turkic case folding and whitespace collapsing. Both locate literal occurrences, not semantic equivalents; result ranges use original-source UTF-16 positions. |
| `order` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["document","reverse_document"]; default="document" | document (default) returns earliest occurrences first; reverse_document returns latest first. Uses canonical source order, not lexical LID sorting. Combine before with reverse_document for previous occurrences. |
| `page_size` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=20; minimum=1; maximum=50 | Maximum literal occurrences per search page, 1..50, default 20. Does not change total_occurrences or exhaustive coverage of the requested literal scope; preserve it when using cursor. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | Literal text to locate in canonical source, containing non-whitespace text and at most 4,096 Unicode scalar values. Exact mode preserves query whitespace; this is not a semantic question or regex. Read chosen hits using their start_lid/end_lid. |
| `scope` | 按分支／可选 | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" | Optional source scope using known LIDs from this book. Omit, null or {} searches the whole book; within_lid and relative_to intersect, and each whole hit must fit the resulting range. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-search_text-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "match_mode": "exact",
    "order": "document",
    "page_size": 1,
    "query": "threshold"
  },
  {
    "match_mode": "normalized",
    "order": "reverse_document",
    "page_size": 1,
    "query": "THRESHOLD",
    "scope": {
      "relative_to": {
        "direction": "before",
        "lid": "1.3"
      },
      "within_lid": "1"
    }
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

<a id="resident-book-structure"></a>

### book.structure

Project BookStructure to explain a LID's role in the whole-book spine, throughline, and key stops. Without at, return the whole-book overview; when the sidecar is absent, return explicit unavailable status.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/17`。

**适用**：Inspect the structural role of a LID or the whole-book overview.

**避免**：Do not use as a source-text search surface.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `at` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID in this book to locate its structural role or current route segment. Omit or null for the whole-book view; this is a location, not a natural-language query. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-structure-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json

  {
    "at": "1.2"
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-book-synthesize"></a>

### book.synthesize

Synthesize a caller-supplied discrete LID set without expanding retrieval, returning an answer whose citations are a subset of the input LIDs.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/18`。

**适用**：Synthesize a caller-supplied, frozen set of LIDs.

**避免**：Do not expand evidence beyond the supplied LIDs.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`high`。

**交付策略**：`QueryResponse`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": false,
  "openWorldHint": true,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lids` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1 | Nonempty list of known LIDs selected as the complete evidence scope. Synthesis reads only these nodes and returns citations drawn from this list, without expanding retrieval. |
| `task` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Synthesis instruction for the supplied LIDs; omit or null to synthesize their content without an additional task. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-synthesize-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "lids": [
      "1.1",
      "1.2"
    ],
    "task": "Summarize these two threshold conditions using only these passages; state any evidence gaps."
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-book-text"></a>

### book.text

Read canonical source text by one LID or an inclusive LID range. Optional math_context annotates the delivered passage and supplies next_calls for missing statement evidence and referenced results; annotations and calls do not observe unread text.

**来源符号**：[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/19`。

**适用**：Read source text after a LID has been located.

**避免**：Do not use for lexical discovery when no LID is known.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `end_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Inclusive final LID in source reading order; for a search hit, use end_lid. Reads from lid's span start through end_lid's span end. Omit or null to read only lid's span. |
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | Known LID from this book's locating or structure results; for a search hit, use start_lid. Reads the node's canonical source span, including descendants. |
| `start_utf16` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=0 | Absolute canonical-source UTF-16 position copied from a text continuation. Keep the original lid/end_lid. Omit or null for the initial read; a continuation returns a bounded page and its source_range, requested_range and next_start_utf16. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-text-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "end_lid": "1.2",
    "lid": "1.1"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `model_body` | anyOf: type="object"; oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=true / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / 任意 JSON 值 |
| `model_body.requested_range` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false — 内容区间(半开 [start, end)),映射回源文。 切片0 = **UTF-16 code unit 下标**(TS segment 用 JS 串下标);字节精确化留后 `[ADR-0024]`。 ⇒ Rust book.text 须按 UTF-16 语义切原文(encode_utf16 索引),勿按 UTF-8 字节直切(中文会错位)。 |
| `model_body.source_range` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false — 内容区间(半开 [start, end)),映射回源文。 切片0 = **UTF-16 code unit 下标**(TS segment 用 JS 串下标);字节精确化留后 `[ADR-0024]`。 ⇒ Rust book.text 须按 UTF-16 语义切原文(encode_utf16 索引),勿按 UTF-8 字节直切(中文会错位)。 |
| `status` | const="ok" / const="partial" / const="error" / type="string"; enum=["ok","partial","error"] |
| `truncated` | const=false / type="boolean" |
| `model_body.delivery.status` | const="omitted" / const="partial" |
| `continuation` | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false |
| `receipt.status` | type="string"; enum=["ok","error","legacy_unparsed"] |
| `version` | const="tool_result_envelope.v1" |

<a id="resident-book-text_batch"></a>

### book.text_batch

Read several known source ranges together, preserving each range separately. For a continuous argument, sequence or case, use its known lid and end_lid to read the complete relevant relationship, not just isolated matching sentences or titles. Locate only missing endpoints with context or search before reading; never guess LIDs. Prefer one batch for independent ranges; use book.text for a single range. Only delivered text is evidence; follow next_start_utf16 when the needed scope continues beyond the returned text.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/20`。

**适用**：Read several already-located source ranges together for comparison or evidence gathering; the runtime enables this tool after multiple source locations are known.

**避免**：Do not guess LIDs or use batch reads to locate unknown evidence; use book.text for one range.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ranges` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=8 | One to eight known source ranges, in requested order. Exact duplicate requests are read once. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-book-text_batch-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

当前定义未发布输入示例。

|---|---|
| `model_body` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=true / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" / 任意 JSON 值 |
| `status` | const="ok" / const="partial" / const="error" / type="string"; enum=["ok","partial","error"] |
| `truncated` | const=false / type="boolean" |

| `version` | const="tool_result_envelope.v1" |

<a id="resident-book-unvisited_back"></a>

### book.unvisited_back

Structural fallback for an unspecific statement of non-understanding: return unread prerequisites at the current LID, defined as the deterministic intersection of route_from(at).back and unread locations. Use it only when the user gives no locus, example request, relationship target, or requested prerequisite. A nonempty result starts with the best prerequisite to revisit; an empty result means to re-explain in place. Zero LLM; every LID is real.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/21`。

**适用**：Find unread prerequisites for an unspecific statement of non-understanding.

**避免**：Do not call when the user named a specific locus, example, relation, or prerequisite.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`、`located_lid`、`current_reader_state`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`NavigationProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `at` | 是 | type="string" | Current LID |

<a id="resident-book-unvisited_back-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

<a id="resident-goal-update"></a>

### goal.update

Update the active Resident task only when interpretation, requirements or working state changes. working saves concise results in focus, remaining knowledge or understanding gaps in open_questions, the next concrete action in next_move, and optionally replaces the plan; refine sets interpretation/requirements only in the goal's origin turn; revise changes requirements using the current user turn ID and an exact quote from that message. Replace example turn IDs with actual IDs from the active Goal; each requirement must cite the origin or current user turn. Working items record a revisable plan, not user requirements. Keep the plan consistent with actual results and remaining content gaps before finishing. Success returns {goal_id, revision, changed}; changed=false means no state change. This tool cannot mark delivery or Goal completion; completed items do not prove either. Short direct answers need no update or plan. Use working in a separate call to save focus, next_move or plan items; refine/revise do not accept these fields.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/22`。

**适用**：Use working for focus/next_move/plan; refine requirements in the origin turn; revise with the current user's exact quote.

**避免**：Do not mix plan fields into refine/revise, replace user requirements with a method, or claim completion from a Goal update.

**效果与前提**：effect=`goal_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryReceipt`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `basis_quote` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `basis_turn_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `interpretation` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `items` | 按分支／可选 | type=["array","null"] | For operation=working: replace the whole ordered work plan atomically. Omit to retain it; [] clears it. Use non-empty unique IDs and descriptions naming concrete outcomes and, when necessary, what evidence completes them. Keep valid results and stable identities. Update after meaningful stages, not every small tool action. Reorder, split, merge, remove abandoned steps or reopen completed items as observations require; this does not change requirements. |
| `next_move` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `open_questions` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["working","refine","revise"] | — |
| `requirements` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
[
  {
    "additionalProperties": false,
    "properties": {
      "focus": {},
      "items": {},
      "next_move": {},
      "open_questions": {},
      "operation": {
        "enum": [
          "working"
        ]
      }
    },
    "required": [
      "operation",
      "focus",
      "next_move"
    ]
  },
  {
    "additionalProperties": false,
    "properties": {
      "interpretation": {},
      "operation": {
        "enum": [
          "refine"
        ]
      },
      "requirements": {}
    },
    "required": [
      "operation",
      "interpretation",
      "requirements"
    ]
  },
  {
    "additionalProperties": false,
    "properties": {
      "basis_quote": {},
      "basis_turn_id": {},
      "interpretation": {},
      "operation": {
        "enum": [
          "revise"
        ]
      },
      "requirements": {}
    },
    "required": [
      "operation",
      "basis_turn_id",
      "basis_quote",
      "interpretation",
      "requirements"
    ]
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-goal-update-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "focus": "核对当前段落的解释依据",
    "items": [
      {
        "description": "核对原文并解释",
        "id": "explain",
        "status": "in_progress"
      },
      {
        "description": "保存用户要求的笔记并核对回执",
        "id": "note",
        "status": "pending"
      }
    ],
    "next_move": "解释后保存用户要求的笔记",
    "operation": "working"
  },
  {
    "interpretation": "解释当前段落并保存用户要求的笔记",
    "operation": "refine",
    "requirements": [
      {
        "basis_turn_id": "origin-user-turn-id",
        "description": "解释当前段落",
        "id": "explain",
        "verification": "content"
      },
      {
        "basis_turn_id": "origin-user-turn-id",
        "description": "在当前段落保存笔记",
        "id": "note",
        "verification": "reader_action"
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "basis_quote": "只解释，不要保存笔记",
    "basis_turn_id": "current-user-turn-id",
    "interpretation": "只解释当前段落",
    "operation": "revise",
    "requirements": [
      {
        "basis_turn_id": "current-user-turn-id",
        "description": "只解释当前段落，不保存笔记",
        "id": "explain",
        "verification": "content"
      }
    ]
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

当前定义未发布 outputSchema；结果／效果按以上工具说明、路由元数据和原执行回执解释。

<a id="resident-library-learning"></a>

### library.learning

Read or update this reader's private understanding of a reference material within this run's learning workspace. Understanding and superseding evidence stay in this workspace. Read before supplementing an explanation. observe records an unassessed hypothesis from an exact quote of the CURRENT user's words and source_ref_ids registered this turn for that publication. Use supersedes to revise an earlier hypothesis. Assistant explanations, UI action labels and mere reading are not evidence of mastery. Never assign a score. Actual delivered help is recorded automatically after commit.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/23`。

**适用**：Read this learner's reference-book understanding and record source-grounded interpretations of actual user feedback.

**避免**：Private learning memory belongs to the current reader. Read, explain, then update only from the user's real expression; no automatic mastery.

**效果与前提**：effect=`memory_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | 无具名参数 | 见完整 schema |

<a id="resident-library-list"></a>

### library.list

Find materials in this run's learning workspace that are currently authorized for this reader when a specific knowledge gap needs supplementary reading. Returns names, exact published_book_ref values and available operations. Copy the reference into library.read. The current book and Reader position stay unchanged. Directory entries are not evidence; follow next_calls for further pages.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/24`。

**适用**：Find supplementary source material in the reader's authorized library for a specific knowledge gap.

**避免**：Keep the original reading task; do not treat other materials as current-book navigation or infer understanding from a read.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `after` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1; maximum=50 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-library-list-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {}
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

当前定义未发布 outputSchema；结果／效果按以上工具说明、路由元数据和原执行回执解释。

<a id="resident-library-read"></a>

### library.read

Read a selected material from this run's frozen workspace members while current account authorization remains valid, without switching books. Choose structure/concept/search_text/context to locate relevant passages, then text to read evidence. Use the same canonical Book parameters with operation and published_book_ref. Only actually returned source text supports an explanation. Follow returned next_calls unchanged. Consult this reader's understanding of the selected material; do not treat another book's LIDs as current-book navigation targets.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/25`。

**适用**：Find supplementary source material in the reader's authorized library for a specific knowledge gap.

**避免**：Keep the original reading task; do not treat other materials as current-book navigation or infer understanding from a read.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,

}
```

<a id="resident-library-search"></a>

### library.search

Locate relevant passages across this run's frozen learning-workspace materials, filtered by current account authorization. Uses lexical relevance and, when configured, semantic retrieval; the result reports the actual retrieval status. published_book_refs may only narrow the workspace. Results are bounded candidates, never exhaustive coverage or observed source evidence. Follow each hit's library.read text call before explaining or citing. For an explicit selection read it directly; for one ordinary article prefer book.search without embeddings.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/26`。

**适用**：Find related passages across frozen workspace materials with lexical and available semantic retrieval, then follow returned source reads.

**避免**：Requires a learning workspace. Search previews are not read evidence; bounded results cannot establish complete coverage. For one article prefer book.search or direct reading.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`medium`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1; maximum=20 | — |
| `published_book_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array"; minItems=1 | — |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-memory-save"></a>

### memory.save

Save an existing reader fact at a verified anchor_lid. type=highlight stores supplied content as long_term memory without resolving a source range or selecting it in Reader; use reader.highlight for a requested source highlight. position stores a session reading-position memory, without navigating. qa stores the user's original substantive question after answering with book.query, not the generated answer or routine chat history. context stores sourced reader background, preferences, interests or sticking points; retain what the user actually said and distinguish inference. Use reader.note for every note. context needs no book citation for a user-stated preference; citations may contain real supporting LIDs, and invalid LIDs are dropped. Success returns the saved record {mem_id,type,layer,anchor,content,citations,...}; it is not a note_id or proof of learning. Replace example locations and content with observed facts; do not write merely to explain selected text.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/28`。

**适用**：Save sourced reader context, an original substantive question, supplied highlight content or a position memory at a verified anchor.

**避免**：Use reader.note for notes and reader.highlight for exact source highlighting. Do not write for an explanation alone or present an inference as a user fact.

**效果与前提**：effect=`memory_write`；preconditions=`memory_write_permission`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryReceipt`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anchor_lid` | 是 | type="string" | — |
| `citations` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Optional real LIDs supporting this memory, primarily for context; invalid LIDs are dropped and the list may be empty |
| `content` | 是 | type="string" | — |
| `type` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["highlight","position","qa","context"] | — |

```json
  {
    "anchor_lid": "1.1",

    "anchor_lid": "1.1",
    "citations": [],
    "content": "用户说：我有 Rust 基础，读这一段时请先给例子",
    "type": "context"
  }
]
```
