<a id="resident-music-example"></a>

### music.example

Register playable examples and computed score facts. Read both endpoints with music.read first. kind=score selects one part/voice from from_id through through_id inclusive, retaining intervening notes, chords, ties and rests (at most 200 objects). It returns a listen marker and facts_marker. Insert facts_marker as a standalone paragraph to display host-computed pitches, durations and endpoint span verbatim; explain their significance in your prose. kind=comparison takes left/right selections and returns both playable examples plus one facts_marker with both passages and an onset/rhythm/pitch comparison. If rhythmic structures differ, choose explicit corresponding subranges instead of assuming note correspondence. A constant pitch-class shift may still involve different octave changes. These calculations do not establish thematic derivation. Batch a few short passages per call. kind=illustration makes an explicitly invented example from pitch names with octaves and quarter-note durations; empty pitches is rest. Insert listen markers inline where the sound belongs. Registration never starts playback; the fixed piano tempo is quarter=66 and tremolo strokes are not expanded.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/29`。

**适用**：Create inline playable piano examples from exact score objects or explicit teaching pitches. 音乐 钢琴 试听 音阶 和弦

**避免**：Read score endpoints first and insert returned listen markers in the final answer. Registration never starts playback.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `items` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=24 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-music-interact"></a>

### music.interact

Observe the open music player, then request one discrete play/pause/seek/loop/focus/rate/synthesized demonstration action using based_on from the latest receipt. Match publication and recording exactly. play with start_ms or end_ms is a single passage and clears an existing loop; play with neither field resumes and preserves the current loop. loop only sets the range and does not start playback: to start a loop, set loop then use play with neither field, each based_on the latest receipt. For a requested short listen, normally use one play(start_ms,end_ms), without a loop. Before the sound starts, give the learner an audible focus by setting cue_event_id to an event already read this turn; the browser displays that published event's listen_for text verbatim before play or demonstration, and the receipt reports displayed_cue. Do not substitute agent.progress for teaching content. An action is completed only when the returned actual browser receipt says applied; requests, timeouts, failed playback and elapsed time never establish completion. A playing receipt does not mean the learner heard or understood, or that the model received audio. Focus highlights complete parts or explicit voices (part_ids OR voices; omit that whole part when selecting only one voice); it does not extract or mute instruments in the original recording. Demonstration uses prepared score synthesis, clearly labelled as such. Leave quiet time for listening and honor user pause or direction changes.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/30`。

**适用**：Observe and control the current music player: play, pause, seek, loop, focus, speed or labelled synthesis. 音乐 播放 暂停 循环 声部 高亮

**避免**：Observe before acting; only an applied browser receipt confirms completion. Playback is not proof of learner understanding or model hearing.

**效果与前提**：effect=`presentation_write`；preconditions=`book_available`、`reader_write_permission`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | 按分支／可选 | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `based_on` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | — |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["observe","act"] | — |
| `publication` | 是 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `recording` | 是 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
  {
    "not": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "required": [
            "action"
          ]
        },
        {
          "required": [
            "based_on"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "properties": {
      "operation": {
        "enum": [
          "observe"
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  {
    "properties": {
      "operation": {
        "enum": [
          "act"
        ]
      }
    },
    "required": [
      "based_on",
      "action"
    ]
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-music-read"></a>

### music.read

Read published music overview, compact catalog, events or score objects, with provenance and original recording/score anchors. This reads prepared music analysis, not audio; never describe it as personally hearing the recording. Overview gives instruments, structure, a compact material catalog and coverage. Catalog supports discovery without reading or citing all event details. Read an event_id from the catalog to obtain its full analysis and evidence. Catalog/event query terms match independently and are ranked. Score note_pairs gives computed written intervals and duration ratios; notation retains visibility, spelling and playback dynamics. Events supports query/event_id/range/part filters; score returns notes, key/time signatures, tempo/direction marks, inherited notation context and alignment including separate repeated occurrences. Score query also searches mark text, such as Allegro. Raw encoded_mode and MusicXML playback tempo remain distinct from reviewed work_tonality and measured recording tempo. Score facts are usable independently of audio-alignment coverage. Score pages preserve complete part/voice measures. Select a single voice and a short range, normally limit=64; inspect returned_scope and range_complete, and follow next_offset when present. Explicit source_ids/query matches may be partial measures. Use music.example for complete passage facts and comparisons. Copy sources[].target into source.present music_target to cite the read snapshot, including its notation facts and tonality provenance. The input-time music question remains fixed when playback advances.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/31`。

**适用**：Read prepared music, score notation, part roles, events, provenance, alignment and coverage. 音乐 乐谱 乐器 声部 主题 伴奏 贝多芬 舒伯特 伴听

**避免**：Prepared material is not live audio observation. Bind original recording and score revisions; preserve uncertainty and coverage.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `event_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=20; minimum=1; maximum=200 | — |
| `location` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | — |
| `part_ids` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `published_book_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `query` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; maxLength=512 | Catalog/events: whitespace-separated terms match independently and are ranked. Score: a source locator or mark-text substring. An empty query lists materials. |
| `source_ids` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `view` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["overview","catalog","events","score"]; default="overview" | — |
| `voices` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Specific voices; part_ids selects all voices in a part, and voices selects only named voices. Use empty part_ids when focusing one voice of that part. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

Read a personal thought and its explicit later expressions and links. Omit revision to inspect today's version; provide revision to read the exact version selected or discussed. Continue long text with start_char. Current conversation corrections take precedence; this read does not create mastery facts.

**避免**：Keep authorship and temporal uncertainty. The current user message overrides older expressions; never turn recall into a new learning fact.

<a id="resident-presentation-author"></a>

### presentation.author

Create or revise rich answers. prepare replaces the complete run-local phase/framework/focus/needs; phase and needs are required. Omitted framework/focus clear previous values. Call prepare alone; selected guidance applies at the next sampling. It does not create or deliver content. read/search/patch require exactly one delivered reference or current-run candidate_id. read returns a file or offset/length range; search returns literal matches and character offsets. patch applies ordered edits, each old_text matching exactly once, atomically producing a new immutable candidate while preserving other content/resources; optional metadata replaces only supplied fields. write saves a complete candidate and requires title, html and readable_content; based_on selects an exact delivered version, never a candidate. render_plot returns a static SVG asset; render_animation returns a fixed movie asset. Include returned asset_refs and selected libraries on every write. preview executes actions and returns candidate/environment-bound DOM, errors and screenshots; width and viewport are mutually exclusive. deliver saves only a candidate meeting the preview and subsequent-observation contract; changed candidates need new previews. read_method reads a registered explanation method by exact method_id and revision, without a presentation reference or candidate. Default file is card.md; the result lists the entrypoint and all registered files. Read only selected files/ranges; source is returned on demand and grants no delivery eligibility. Examples illustrate calls, not live object IDs: replace reference/based_on with the exact delivered reference, candidate_id with the current run's write/patch result, and source/asset refs and asset paths with actual observed results. For an edit chain, read/search the delivered reference, patch it, then read/search/patch the returned candidate before preview and deliver. Observe each preview in a later sampling before deliver; explicit viewport previews must cover all environments required by the returned receipt.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/presentation_author.rs:spec](../crates/runtime/src/presentation_author.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/34`。

**适用**：Create and rehearse rich HTML answers, static Python/Matplotlib charts and interactive explanations.

**避免**：Do not use for a short plain answer or persist an untested candidate.

**效果与前提**：effect=`presentation_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`high`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action_contract` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Declared live actions. Parameters refer to state_contract and real controls marked data-presentation-parameter; invoke binds a real control marked data-presentation-action. Regions use data-teaching-region or id. Register registerActionHandler; reuse control functions and await actual rendering. annotate only reveals existing reviewed content. |
| `actions` | 按分支／可选 | type="array"; maxItems=4 | — |
| `asset_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Refs returned by render_plot/render_animation or listed by read; include each asset used by html. |
| `assumptions` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `based_on` | 按分支／可选 | type=["object","null"]; additionalProperties=false | write: exact delivered revision to revise. With a presentation follow-up receipt, must match its reference. Omit only for a new object; a follow-up then requires new_object=true. |
| `candidate_id` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Current-run candidate returned by write/patch. Use this field for read/search/patch/preview/deliver; do not wrap it in reference. |
| `code` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | render_plot: statements using plt/fig/ax/data. render_animation: define PresentationAnimation(Scene), with Manim symbols and JSON data available. |
| `cues` | 按分支／可选 | type="array"; maxItems=16 | — |
| `data` | 按分支／可选 | 见完整 schema | JSON data available as the Python variable data |
| `edits` | 按分支／可选 | type="array"; minItems=1 | patch: ordered exact replacements in the entrypoint; every old_text must match once. All edits succeed or no candidate is saved. Unchanged bytes and version resources are preserved. |
| `file` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | read_method: registered relative file name, defaults to card.md; read the listed entrypoint and adaptation guide separately. read/search: logical file name; defaults to entrypoint. Use readable_content for the version's explanatory prose with offset/length or query, without HTML. Omit file to read entrypoint source plus full readable_content metadata. Managed libraries return metadata only. |
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | prepare: current local purpose and its relation to the whole. Omission clears the previous focus. |
| `framework` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | prepare: brief current whole-work design, as task data. Omission clears the previous framework. |
| `html` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `initial_state` | 按分支／可选 | 见完整 schema | — |
| `length` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=1 | read: exact requested range length; omitted reads to file end. read_method: page size in Unicode characters, within model output budget; end_offset is the actual returned page end and next_offset continues through the whole file, null only at EOF. Omit to request the remaining file. |
| `libraries` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Optional fixed host libraries. Explicitly select dependencies on every write, including based_on revisions; omitted means none. |
| `max_matches` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=1; maximum=50 | search: default 20. Follow next_offset to find subsequent occurrences. |
| `method_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | read_method: exact id from the method directory. |
| `method_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | write/patch: complete list of methods actually used, with exact method_id/revision and optional local scope. New references require method source observed in a previous sampling; inherited references need no reread. patch omission inherits, a supplied list replaces all references ([] clears). write never implicitly inherits; when rewriting a version with recorded methods, explicitly declare the full list, including [] if none remain. |
| `needs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Required for prepare, including []: technical guidance needed for the next sampling. |
| `new_object` | 按分支／可选 | type="boolean" | write: true explicitly creates a separate presentation, including from a follow-up. Use only when the user wants a new object. Cannot be true together with based_on. Defaults to false; without a follow-up or based_on, ordinary creation is unchanged. |
| `offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | read/read_method/search: zero-based Unicode character offset (not bytes or UTF-16). read: follow next_offset with the remaining requested range length when output is truncated. read_method: follow the returned next_offset with the same page size until null (EOF); do not guess or skip offsets. search: follow next_offset for subsequent matches. |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["prepare","render_plot","render_animation","read","read_method","search","patch","write","preview","deliver"] | — |
| `phase` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["global","local","review"] | Required for prepare: current design responsibility. |
| `query` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | search: literal case-sensitive source text, not a regular expression. |
| `read_selector` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | preview: CSS selector matching one visible result region. After the final action, returns its complete rendered text together with live page state, input values and scene position as reading. Use this page-computed reading for numerical claims; keep each value with its parameters and semantic step. action_step counts preview actions, not mathematical iterations. For another state, run another preview with the required actions. A playing scene must be paused/seeked first. Narrow the selected region if its full context exceeds the result budget. |
| `readable_content` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | — |
| `reference` | 按分支／可选 | type=["object","null"]; additionalProperties=false | read/search/patch: exact saved reference returned by deliver. For a write/patch candidate use candidate_id instead; never put a candidate ID in presentation_id. |
| `revision` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | read_method: exact method revision from the directory; no implicit latest version. |
| `size` | 按分支／可选 | type=["object","null"]; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `source_ref_ids` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | write: list every source ref used in HTML or readable_content. patch preserves existing source bindings; use write to change them. |
| `state_contract` | 按分支／可选 | type=["object","null"] | Parameter name -&gt; semantic definition string, including units and allowed range. Preserve a definition only when old values retain exactly the same meaning and domain. Saved page parameters with identical definitions and JSON types replace matching initial_state fields; all others keep new defaults. |
| `teaching_binding` | 按分支／可选 | type=["object","null"]; additionalProperties=false | For active Tutor write/patch, bind this exact page version to the accepted current design_ref and block_refs. regions map real id or data-teaching-region attributes to relationships, state_fields and the meaningful observable change. Keep object identity stable across conditions; a static representation is valid. prepare may omit this field to use the current design focus. A changed teaching design requires an explicit current binding when revising a candidate. |
| `title` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | — |
| `viewport` | 按分支／可选 | type=["object","null"]; additionalProperties=false | Explicit content-container environment. Do not provide width at the same time. |
| `width` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=240; maximum=1920 | Preview width in CSS pixels; defaults to 960 with height 720 and mouse input. Counts only for that actual environment, never the required touch viewports. Mutually exclusive with viewport. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-presentation-interact"></a>

### presentation.interact

Observe or operate the exact delivered presentation currently open in the Reader. A board_target addresses the retained item at the stated board revision, including content from another or deleted chat; it does not grant content authoring rights. Observe the current page first; act uses its saved_state_ref and declared actions, never a frozen question snapshot. Only applied with a saved receipt confirms completion. Chain a local demonstration from each actual receipt, leaving the same page available for learner edits. teaching.action_ref records teacher usage and teaching.assistance_refs identify the visible help saved with that scene; these are not learner answers or independent mastery. On conflict, cancellation or timeout observe again; never automatically retry an action. Parameters change runtime state, not the content version.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/35`。

**适用**：Observe and operate a declared action in the currently open presentation, then read its saved result.

**避免**：Do not use to modify content or claim learner understanding; use only the returned actions and exact observation receipt.

**效果与前提**：effect=`presentation_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | 按分支／可选 | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `based_on` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `board_target` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["observe","act"] | — |
| `reference` | 是 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
      "reference"
    ]
  },
  {
    "additionalProperties": false,
    "properties": {
      "action": {},
      "based_on": {},
      "board_target": {},
      "operation": {
        "enum": [
          "act"
        ]
      },
      "reference": {}
    },
    "required": [
      "operation",
      "reference",
      "based_on",
      "action"
    ]
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-profile-manifest"></a>

### profile.manifest

Return the current book's ProfileManifest. Optionally set profile_id to technical_learning or paper to read that explicit registry manifest.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/36`。

**适用**：Read profile slots, presets, projections, or tool policy needed for the current task.

**避免**：Do not read a profile manifest when the frozen profile snapshot is already sufficient.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`profile_read_permission`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ProfileProjection`，model_body 上限 12288 字节；并行策略 `ReadOnlyEligible`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `profile_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["technical_learning","paper"] | Optional; defaults to the current book profile |

<a id="resident-profile-mark_used"></a>

### profile.mark_used

Optionally declare read-only profile use. Report only fact IDs from this turn's snapshot that actually affected the answer, together with their influence dimensions. This tool neither reads nor changes memory.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/37`。

**适用**：Trace profile facts that actually changed the current retrieval or answer.

**避免**：Do not claim use of facts absent from the frozen profile snapshot.

**效果与前提**：effect=`profile_usage_trace`；preconditions=`profile_snapshot_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ProfileUsageReceipt`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fact_ids` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1 | — |
| `influences` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-reader-gotoLid"></a>

### reader.gotoLid

Jump to a LID. A leaf anchors to itself; a container anchors to the first leaf in its subtree. Returns the updated viewport {anchor_lid, visible_lids}.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/38`。

**适用**：Perform an explicitly requested or explicitly authorized Reader jump to a verified LID.

**避免**：Do not mutate the Reader for a read-only request or jump to an unverified LID.

**效果与前提**：effect=`reader_write`；preconditions=`reader_write_permission`、`explicit_reader_mutation_intent`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ReaderEffect`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | Target LID |

<a id="resident-reader-highlight"></a>

### reader.highlight

Save a user-requested source highlight at a verified LID. For a sentence, pass the exact unique quote within that LID; omit quote only for the whole passage. This interface accepts one lid and an optional quote, not offsets, an end_lid or note text. Use reader.note for commentary. Success returns {ok:true,highlight_id}, the saved session memory ID. If reader_effect.status=not_applied, persistence succeeded but the Reader selection did not apply. Replace example locations and quotes with observed source text; an explanation alone needs no highlight.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/39`。

**适用**：Save an explicitly requested source highlight: exact unique quote or the whole verified LID.

**避免**：Use reader.note for note text. Do not highlight for an explanation alone or claim Reader selection when reader_effect is not_applied.

**效果与前提**：effect=`reader_write`；preconditions=`reader_write_permission`、`explicit_reader_mutation_intent`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ReaderEffect`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | — |
| `quote` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Exact requested original text within this LID; must match once |

<a id="resident-reader-note"></a>

### reader.note

Save the user's requested note text at a verified source lid. The lid is the note's body placement; text is the content to retain, not a highlight quote. Use this tool for notes, including requested summaries of an explanation; memory.save cannot create notes. Success returns {ok:true,note_id,status:CREATED|EXISTING}; note_id is the persisted memory ID. EXISTING reuses the same note rather than creating another. New Agent notes are saved in the session layer. If reader_effect.status=not_applied, the note is saved but Reader selection did not apply. Replace the example lid with the actual requested location. Do not create a note for an explanation-only request or claim success before the receipt.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/41`。

**适用**：Save user-requested note text at a verified source LID and confirm note_id with CREATED/EXISTING status.

**避免**：Do not use memory.save for notes, save for an explanation alone, or claim persistence before a successful receipt.

**效果与前提**：effect=`reader_write`；preconditions=`reader_write_permission`、`explicit_reader_mutation_intent`、`located_lid`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ReaderEffect`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | — |
| `text` | 是 | type="string" | Note content |

<a id="resident-source-present"></a>

### source.present

Convert contiguous in-book evidence already observed in this turn into a user-visible source reference. For prepared music pass music_target copied from music.read sources[].target; it cites the exact score/analysis snapshot and never claims a live audio observation. For text pass quote; for a reference book also pass its exact published_book_ref returned by library.read; for video pass target (text, visual, audio, or joint) copied from actual reads, with each joint member's support_role. Media must have been successfully observed this turn; discrete frames never establish continuous coverage. For quote: copy the original text exactly, including punctuation, whitespace and formulas. The tool prefers exact text, but also accepts whitespace changes immediately inside paired $ or $$ formula delimiters; formula contents and prose remain exact. It locates the quote within observed source and computes original LIDs and character ranges; do not supply LIDs. Select enough text to support the complete claim, including necessary conditions, negation and qualifications; cite non-adjacent premises separately. Keywords and headings locate evidence; read the body before explaining a mechanism or inference. SOURCE_AMBIGUOUS means the text occurs at multiple observed positions: extend quote with distinctive observed context. SOURCE_NOT_OBSERVED means no supported match: correct an identifiable copying error, otherwise reopen the original passage and copy the required contiguous text from that result. Do not cycle through guessed spellings, whitespace variants or shorter fragments without identifying the actual source; this is a reading gap, not a reason to keep trying guesses. Returns an opaque source_ref_id, label and preview. Inspect the preview: if it is only a word or heading but the claim needs a passage, submit the supporting passage. The preview may be truncated; missing text in the preview alone does not mean the binding failed.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/45`。

**适用**：Present an opaque user-visible source reference for evidence already observed in this turn.

**避免**：Pass verbatim observed quote or a media target copied from successful observations; the tool resolves its location. Do not use source presentation as evidence retrieval.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`observed_turn_evidence`、`source_presentation_permission`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`SourceReference`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `music_target` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `published_book_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `quote` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | Continuous verbatim text already observed this turn; include enough context to support the claim and uniquely identify the passage. The tool computes LIDs and exact offsets. |
| `target` | 按分支／可选 | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
[
  {
    "required": [
      "quote"
    ]
  },
  {
    "required": [
      "target"
    ]
  },
  {
    "required": [
      "music_target"
    ]
  }
]
```

```json

  {
    "quote": "The alpha threshold activates only when the value is greater than 10."
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-tool-search"></a>

### tool.search

Find Resident tools by required capabilities, scope, operation, and authorized effect mode. Natural-language task text ranks only candidates that pass Runtime gates; matches never execute a target tool, and newly activated deferred tools become visible in the next sampling.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:tool_search_input_schema_v2](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/46`。

**适用**：Discover a deferred Resident capability that is absent from the current sampled tool surface.

**避免**：Do not use when visible tools can complete the task or to execute a matched tool in the same sampling.

**效果与前提**：effect=`tool_activation`；preconditions=`deferred_capability_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`ToolDiscovery`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `effect_mode` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["read_only","reader_mutation_explicitly_requested"] | — |
| `max_results` | 是 | type="integer"; minimum=1; maximum=6 | — |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["locate_literal","read_source","explain","compare","summarize","navigate","mutate_reader"] | — |
| `required_capabilities` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=22 | — |
| `scope` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["selection","passage","section","document","corpus"] | — |
| `task` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=512 | Natural-language task used only to rank already-authorized candidates |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-tool-search-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
  {
    "effect_mode": "read_only",
    "max_results": 2,
    "operation": "summarize",

      "structural_index"

  {

      "lexical_locate"

  },
  {
    "effect_mode": "read_only",
    "max_results": 1,
    "operation": "read_source",
    "required_capabilities": [
      "source_read"
    ],
    "scope": "passage",
    "task": "Read the passage selected from the search results"
  },
  {
    "effect_mode": "reader_mutation_explicitly_requested",
    "max_results": 2,
    "operation": "mutate_reader",
    "required_capabilities": [
      "reader_write"
    ],

]
```

<a id="resident-tutor-step"></a>

### tutor.step

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/tutor.rs:spec/spec_for_context](../crates/runtime/src/tutor.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/47`。

**适用**：Read teaching materials and learning evidence, assess a formal response against its frozen contract, and select one grounded move.

**避免**：Do not invent grades or mastery, alter frozen criteria, or advance a paused learning session.

**效果与前提**：effect=`memory_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryReceipt`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | An actual response event_id from context or trace. assess requires an unassessed formal submit/revise with a frozen open rubric; it runs one isolated, budgeted evaluator. Ungraded evidence requires a response to an evaluation activity with a formal object and no scoring contract. |
| `after` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | — |
| `block_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `correction` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `correction_action` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["search","read","record","use"] | Teacher-owned corrections across chats and stages, restricted to this reader/workspace/source version. search retrieves candidates by topic (after=candidate offset); read retrieves an exact correction_ref in chunks (after=character offset, follow next); record saves a source-checked correction to your own actual earlier words; use records whether a candidate applies to this request. Never student mastery evidence. Read details_truncated candidates before deciding. |
| `correction_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Exact candidate correction_ref returned by corrections search. |
| `decision` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["apply","not_applicable"] | — |
| `delivery_move_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | select: exact earlier selections from this turn still included in the final support. Omission delivers only the new selection. Superseded plans are not deliveries. |
| `delivery_scope` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | select only, with a real board_context: publish one complete local support while continuing the same request. Requires participation.choice=support, no question, assessment, response_expectation or continuation, and finish omitted or false. The complete original request and actual committed deliveries determine remaining work. Omit for the final complete delivery; accepted parts remain available without copying them into the final body. |
| `design` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `design_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Exact accepted design revision for this interpretation. |
| `draft_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | revise: copy the exact current draft_ref from the private unaccepted text draft. It identifies this run's latest editable version and expires when replaced, accepted or cleared by a new design/outside decision. A review_ref or accepted move reference is not a draft_ref. |
| `edits` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | revise: atomically replace the selected whole paragraphs using the host's editable_paragraphs for this draft_ref, then submit the complete preserved select for host source, version and delivery checks. Every paragraph number refers to the same original draft, not the result of earlier edits. Use [] after reading missing evidence when the current wording is supported. Untouched paragraphs and unnamed fields remain unchanged; use move_changes for supported metadata corrections and do not resend the full body. |
| `event_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Read one exact trace event in bounded JSON text chunks; after is its character offset. Without event_id, after is the trace cursor. |
| `evidence_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Read one evidence interpretation with operation=understanding; after is the character offset. |
| `fact_refs` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | Actual learner-expression event_ids from the current context or trace. UI clicks and assistant deliveries cannot support a hypothesis. |
| `finish` | 按分支／可选 | type="boolean" | select only: true when this exact delivery completes the current request and every requested tool operation is already done. After successful host validation and selection the runtime delivers it immediately, without another model call to copy the text. Omit or use false while other requested work remains. A learner invitation can be the completed delivery boundary; never claim an unperformed operation is complete. |
| `interpretation` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | What the learner actually expressed, with the help received and what remains unconfirmed. Missing detail alone is not a misconception. For nature=hypothesis provide target, fact_refs and teaching_implication; supersedes replaces an earlier unsupported or outdated interpretation. |
| `move` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false; allOf: 见完整 schema / 见完整 schema / 见完整 schema / 见完整 schema / 见完整 schema | — |
| `move_changes` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | revise only: replace the named whole move fields while preserving the draft's body, source target, design, identity and finish. Omitted fields keep their values. Null removes response_expectation or coach_adjustment; participation cannot be removed. Do not also edit public_prompt when replacing/removing response_expectation. Full host select validation still applies. |
| `nature` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["hypothesis"] | — |
| `note_links` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `object_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | — |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["material","trace","design","select","revise","outside","assess","understanding","evidence","corrections"] | — |
| `operation_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | A new identity for this design revision or interpretation; retry the same input with the same identity. |
| `queries` | 按分支／可选 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=6 | Specific topic words or short synonyms from the current question and source language. Space-separated query terms match independently; stored topic phrases also match within a longer query. A match returns candidates, not a verdict of applicability. Follow next to page through matches. |
| `reason` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=1600 | Why this teacher correction does or does not apply under this question's actual conditions. Current explicit request controls teaching style. |
| `response_quote` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Exact learner words from the referenced ungraded source-discrimination response. |
| `sources` | 按分支／可选 | type="array" | — |
| `supersedes` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | An existing evidence_ref being revised, obtained from understanding. |
| `target` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | A source-grounded focus; formal object identity is optional. |
| `teaching_implication` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | A next step justified by the observed performance, help and remaining uncertainty in this task; revise when current evidence changes. It guides support only within its original scope and yields to the current explicit request. Assisted expression does not establish independent application; untested does not mean unable. Do not prescribe repeating source wording or a fixed teaching template. |
| `understanding` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |

A constant speed v covers vT, so matching this journey gives vT = d. With T positive, dividing both sides by T gives v = d/T. In the earlier 10 km journey, 10 minutes moving plus 5 stopped gives T = 15 minutes = 0.25 hours, so the whole-trip average is 10/0.25 = 40 km/h. The stop belongs to that elapsed interval even though it adds no distance.",

<a id="resident-video-read_frames"></a>

### video.read_frames

Read real video frames and overlapping saved English transcript from the selected workspace publication (published_book_ref), or this Run's publication when omitted. User-attached images can be inspected directly without this tool. For the original frozen frame, copy frame_read_hint as the complete arguments. video.search is only needed to locate unknown positions. Supply 1–8 strictly increasing original-millisecond sample times with start_ms <= t < end_ms in a range of at most 60000 ms. Copy exact actual_pts_ms without rounding. Up to 8 frames across calls accompany each model request: status=partial returns available frames plus complete continuation arguments; status=deferred means earlier successful reads filled the batch. Inspect supplied images before continuing. Neither status is a decode failure. Decoding may return a later frame; use returned read.frame for identity. Optional region crops every sample. Each frame includes source_target ready for source.present, preserving its crop. Images become observed after a successful main-model request; samples do not prove continuous coverage. Paths and descriptions are not visual evidence.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/48`。

**适用**：Read actual video frames, crops, original PTS and corresponding English transcript. 视频画面、代码、公式、取帧、裁剪。

**避免**：Samples only; paths and descriptions do not establish a visual observation.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`high`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `location` | 是 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | Range on the original video timeline in milliseconds: [start_ms, end_ms). Copy video identity from the current video context or a returned location. Require 0 &lt;= start_ms &lt; end_ms &lt;= video duration_ms. |
| `published_book_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `region` | 按分支／可选 | type="array"; minItems=4; maxItems=4 | Optional [x,y,width,height] against the full original frame, with top-left origin; x,y &gt;= 0, width,height &gt; 0, x + width &lt;= 1 and y + height &lt;= 1. Applies to every sample. Omit for full frames. Returned frame dimensions remain the original dimensions; image_width/image_height describe the crop. |
| `sampling` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=8 | 1–8 distinct times in strictly increasing order, in original milliseconds. Every t must satisfy location.start_ms &lt;= t &lt; location.end_ms. Do not include end_ms. For [10000,20000), [10000,15433.3125,19000] is valid; 20000 is not. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-video-read_frames-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "location": {
      "end_ms": 1000,
      "start_ms": 0,
      "video": {
        "media_id": "test-video",
        "media_revision": "1"
      }
    },
    "sampling": [
      200
    ]
  },
  {
    "location": {
      "end_ms": 1000,
      "start_ms": 200,
      "video": {
        "media_id": "test-video",
        "media_revision": "1"
      }
    },
    "region": [
      0.1,
      0.2,
      0.5,
      0.4
    ],
    "sampling": [
      200,
      900
    ]
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-video-review_audio"></a>

### video.review_audio

Review a 1–30000 ms clip of original audio from the selected workspace publication (published_book_ref), or this Run's publication when omitted with the configured local Whisper tool. Use a half-open [start_ms,end_ms) range on the original timeline, within video duration. Returns fresh machine-recognized words, original-time estimates, uncertainties, saved transcript for comparison and an audio source target. Use for what the speaker said or unclear terminology. Preserve uncertainties; recognition probabilities are not proof of correctness. Read related frames separately with video.read_frames when needed. The main model receives the review tool result, not audio input. Copy the returned target into source.present to cite it. Does not edit the saved transcript.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/49`。

**适用**：Review short original audio and uncertain spoken words with Whisper. 原音频、听核、复核、讲者措辞、听不清、underflow、overflow。

**避免**：Machine recognition with uncertainties; compare saved transcript and actual frames; never edit source text or claim the main model heard audio.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`high`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `location` | 是 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | Range on the original video timeline in milliseconds: [start_ms, end_ms). Copy video identity from the current video context or a returned location. Require 0 &lt;= start_ms &lt; end_ms &lt;= video duration_ms. |
| `published_book_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `question` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=2000 | Nonblank listening question of at most 2000 characters. Describe the uncertain wording to compare; this text is recorded as review context and is not supplied to Whisper as a recognition prompt. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-video-review_audio-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "location": {
      "end_ms": 1000,
      "start_ms": 0,
      "video": {
        "media_id": "test-video",
        "media_revision": "1"
      }
    },
    "question": "Did the speaker say underflow or overflow?"
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-video-search"></a>

### video.search

Locate visual candidates by visible code/text, Chinese descriptions or changes, or an exact vu_id in the selected workspace publication (published_book_ref), or this Run's publication when omitted. Use concise concepts/identifiers from the question; reformulate descriptive queries when needed. Returns independent occurrences, analysis IDs, coverage gaps and video.read_frames hints. The discourse section also recalls teaching roles and follows explanations, examples or limitations to supporting material; inspect via for the traversed relation and read text_reads with book.text or read_hints with video.read_frames. Visual and discourse candidates have separate counts and pagination using the same limit/offset. Cards are derived pointers, never evidence; read actual frames before citing. Default chooses the newest accepted analysis at each position; analysis_id selects a published analysis explicitly. No match does not establish absence.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/50`。

**适用**：Locate visual units and source-backed teaching roles; follow explanations, examples and limitations to supporting material. 视频视觉检索、讲解结构、解释、示例、反例、条件限定、前文图示、代码、概念、重复画面、覆盖缺口。

**避免**：Candidates only; read discourse text_reads with book.text and read_hints with video.read_frames before source.present. Search unindexed current frames directly with the frame reader.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `analysis_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Optional exact visual analysis ID returned in analysis_ids or a candidate. Omit to use the newest accepted analysis at each position; do not invent an ID. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=8; minimum=1; maximum=20 | Maximum candidates per result section (visual and discourse), 1–20; default 8. |
| `offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=0; minimum=0 | Zero-based offset applied independently to visual and discourse results. Each section has its own next_offset; follow the section you need with query/within/analysis_id unchanged. |
| `published_book_ref` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=512 | Nonblank query of at most 512 characters: visible identifiers/code, a short Chinese or English description, a teaching role, or an exact returned vu_id. Matching is lexical; no matches do not prove absence. |
| `within` | 按分支／可选 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | Optional original-video range [start_ms,end_ms) in milliseconds; start is inclusive, end exclusive, within video duration. Omit to search the full video; the 60000 ms frame-read limit does not apply to search. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-video-search-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "query": "float16"
  },
  {
    "limit": 4,
    "offset": 0,
    "query": "float16",
    "within": {
      "end_ms": 2000,
      "start_ms": 0,
      "video": {
        "media_id": "test-video",
        "media_revision": "1"
      }
    }
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

当前定义未发布 outputSchema；结果／效果按以上工具说明、路由元数据和原执行回执解释。

<a id="resident-web-fetch"></a>

### web.fetch

Read a bounded part of an external page, reusing cached full content. Use its real URL to cite supported claims. Check that content is relevant page text, not a login/error/blocked page; a successful transport does not prove this. offset and next_offset count Unicode characters; follow next_offset only for a needed missing passage. External pages never establish book LIDs. General external mechanisms alone do not establish this project's intent or measured outcomes; those require its own design records or measurements.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/51`。

**适用**：Read the relevant external source and its conditions at a known URL. 网页正文、官方文档、成立条件。

**避免**：Do not duplicate content already read, mistake blocked pages for evidence, or convert external pages into book LIDs.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`medium`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `max_chars` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=6000; minimum=1; maximum=12000 | — |
| `offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=0; minimum=0 | — |
| `refresh` | 按分支／可选 | type="boolean"; default=false | Re-fetch instead of using saved page content; keep false for continuation pages. |
| `url` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=8192 | — |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-web-search"></a>

### web.search

Find external sources for a specific knowledge gap, a missing causal connection or current facts. Explore with neutral queries, then read the relevant primary source. Titles, snippets, summaries and ranking/authority scores are candidate locators, not verified page evidence. Start with 3 results. Reuse results; refresh only when fresh information is needed. Search fees and subsequent model input fees are separate.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/52`。

**适用**：Find external primary sources for a specific knowledge gap, incomplete mechanism, comparison or current fact. 网页搜索、外部资料、机制关系、最新文档。

**避免**：Do not search for confirmation of an assumed conclusion, treat summaries as page evidence, or use ranking scores as proof.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`medium`。

**交付策略**：`EvidenceProjection`，model_body 上限 16384 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `count` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=3; minimum=1; maximum=5 | — |
| `localization` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Optional IP for an explicitly relevant search region; language alone does not determine region. |
| `need_content` | 按分支／可选 | type="boolean"; default=false | Request page content with candidates when needed; missing content still requires web.fetch. |
| `ori_question` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=128 | Optional concise original question to improve relevance; omit private reader information. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=128 | — |
| `refresh` | 按分支／可选 | type="boolean"; default=false | Bypass cached search results only when their freshness matters. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-workspace-goal-update"></a>

### workspace.goal.update

Change the workspace's overarching learning goal only on an explicit current-user redirection. This is distinct from goal.update, which changes a chat delivery plan. basis_quote must be the complete real user message; goal.text must preserve the exact new wording from it. Preserve applicable accepted constraints and include new constraints only as the user's exact words. A temporary question or prerequisite leaves the goal unchanged. expected_revision is effective_goal.revision from workspace_goal.v1. Success returns the accepted new goal; the old teaching stage stops.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/53`。

**适用**：Change the workspace direction using the complete current user message and exact new goal wording.

**避免**：Do not rewrite the accepted goal for a side question, prerequisite, or Agent plan. Stop the old stage after a new goal is accepted.

**效果与前提**：effect=`goal_write`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryReceipt`，model_body 上限 4096 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `basis_quote` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1 | — |
| `expected_revision` | 是 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | — |
| `goal` | 是 | type="object"; additionalProperties=false | The user's accepted wording and explicit constraints, never an Agent summary. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="resident-workspace-goal-update-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "basis_quote": "把工作区目标改为机器学习期末考试冲刺，考试只剩三天。",
    "expected_revision": 1,
    "goal": {
      "constraints": [
        "考试只剩三天"
      ],
      "text": "机器学习期末考试冲刺"
    }
  }
]
```

<a id="resident-workspace-notes"></a>

### workspace.notes

Find, read and organize reusable notes in the current learning workspace. list searches title/content and returns paged summaries; read pages content, provenance and relationships (follow next_offset/next_relation_offset). Create/import/edit/delete only for the user's requested note task; an explanation alone never grants write intent. Agent-written content is marked assistant. Import preserves host-resolved original content and sources. Reuse note_id; updates/deletes require the current expected_revision. Relationship reasons must explain a real connection between notes. No board selection is needed.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/54`。

**适用**：Find and read reusable learning workspace notes, save requested explanations, and organize related notes with explicit reasons. 学习空间笔记、知识卡片、笔记关联、整理理解。

**避免**：Reading or explanation alone does not authorize changes. Do not invent provenance, overwrite a changed revision, attribute Agent wording to the user or claim mastery from a note.

**效果与前提**：effect=`memory_write`；preconditions=`memory_read_permission`、`memory_write_permission`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["list","read","create","import","update","delete","relation_save","relation_delete"] | — |
| `content` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=100000 | — |
| `expected_revision` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | — |
| `from_note_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | — |
| `label` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=120 | — |
| `length` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=4000; minimum=1; maximum=12000 | Read content length in characters; offset is a character offset for read and an item offset for list. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=20; minimum=1; maximum=30 | — |
| `note_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | — |
| `offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=0; minimum=0 | — |
| `query` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; maxLength=512 | — |
| `reason` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4000 | — |
| `relation_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | — |
| `relation_offset` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=0; minimum=0 | Read another page of this note's relationships. |
| `source` | 按分支／可选 | oneOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="object"; additionalProperties=false | — |
| `title` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=240 | — |
| `to_note_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1 | — |

<a id="resident-workspace-records-read"></a>

### workspace.records.read

List, search or read original conversations and learning records in this learning workspace, across its materials, plus explicitly linked historical selections. Without a learning workspace only current-book chats are available. Search matches full original text before pagination, including older turns, facts and interpretation revisions. Read preserves original identities, event positions, times, sources and corrections. Follow next_request; read_request opens a match. These records do not establish current mastery or expand readable library materials.

**来源符号**：[crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs:declared_tool_specs](../crates/runtime/src/orchestrator.rs)；[crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs:ToolRegistration::output_schema/annotations/registration_for](../crates/runtime/src/tool_registry.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/resident/55`。

**适用**：Read original chats, facts and corrections explicitly linked to this workspace when they matter to the current request.

**避免**：Do not treat historical interpretations as current mastery, overwrite original records or infer access to other materials.

**效果与前提**：effect=`read_only`；preconditions=`book_available`；relative_cost=`low`。

**交付策略**：`MemoryProjection`，model_body 上限 8192 字节；并行策略 `SequentialOnly`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `after` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | — |
| `chat_session_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Identity from list/search; required for read, optional to limit search to one permitted chat. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1; maximum=20 | — |
| `operation` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["list","search","read"] | — |
| `query` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=512 | Case-insensitive literal text to search across all permitted history; required for search, optional for read. |
| `record_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Optional exact record identity returned by search/read. |
| `through_seq` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=1 | Optional native chat snapshot position from an earlier result; cannot expand a linked selection. |
| `turn_id` | 按分支／可选 | type="string" | Optional original turn to read, including its learning records. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_concept"></a>

### book_concept

Deterministically recall whole-book concept and entity candidates with match reasons, bounded previews, and complete occurrence LIDs. Video graph candidates also retain distinct object meanings, text/visual/joint support, relations and video.read_frames hints. Read chosen text with book_text and actual video frames before citing. Use when: Discover concept or entity candidates when the user may not know the graph display label. Do not treat previews or graph support as observed evidence, or silently select a homonym. Read chosen occurrence LIDs with book_text and video_graph.read_hints with video.read_frames; pure visual objects have no invented LIDs.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/3`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anchor_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID in this book to rank nearby candidates and previews. Omit or null for unanchored ranking; this does not restrict recall to a subtree. |
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | anyOf: type="string"; enum=["definition","procedure","explanation","example"] / type="null" | Optional kind of source passage to locate for this concept. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; default=12; minimum=1; maximum=50 | Maximum concept/entity candidates, 1..50; omit or null for 12. Inspect matched_count and truncated for candidate coverage; this does not limit occurrences within a candidate. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | Nonblank concept/entity name, alias or descriptive terms for deterministic candidate recall from names and occurrence text. At most 4,096 Unicode scalar values, including surrounding whitespace; not literal occurrence enumeration. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_concept-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "anchor_lid": "1.2",
    "limit": 5,
    "query": "threshold"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `candidates` | type="array" |
| `matched_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Graph candidates matched by this query's recall rules before limit, not all semantic matches in the book or proof that graph construction is complete. |
| `query` | type="string" |
| `reading` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" |
| `returned_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Candidates selected by limit before transport pagination or model-output budgeting. |
| `truncated` | type="boolean" — Whether limit omitted matched candidates; does not describe occurrence coverage. |
| `version` | type="string" |

<a id="local_mcp-book_context"></a>

### book_context

Return context pointers for a LID: near adds direct mathematical relationships in both directions, tree adjacency and local edges; mid adds other concept or entity occurrences; far adds long-range edges. Mathematical links preserve uses, proves and asks_to_prove, with next_calls covering the related statement's evidence. Counts cover only known rule-recalled pointers, not all semantic relations; built structure availability is reported by book_structure. Pointers contain no source text; use book_text to read it. Use when: Expand around a verified LID through deterministic context links. Do not treat pointers as source text.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/4`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `granularity` | 按分支／可选 | default="near"; anyOf: type="string"; enum=["near","mid","far"] / type="null" | Context reach: near includes tree adjacency and local relations; mid adds other concept/entity occurrences; far also adds long-range relations. Omit or null for near. |
| `k` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; default=10; minimum=0 | Maximum number of context pointers returned across all layers. Omit or null for 10; zero returns no items. total_candidates counts deduplicated pointers before k; returned_count and truncated describe selection by k before pagination or output budgeting. Counts pointers, not distinct LIDs, characters or source passages. |
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | Known LID in this book to expand around. Returned items are context pointers; read their LIDs through the source-text tool for evidence. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_context-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "granularity": "near",
    "k": 3,
    "lid": "1.2"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `anchor` | type="string" |
| `items` | type="array" |
| `returned_count` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Pointers selected by k in the canonical result, before transport pagination or the Resident model-output budget is applied. |
| `total_candidates` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Deduplicated pointers recalled by this anchor/granularity's rules before k. Counts relations, not distinct LIDs or all semantic relationships in the book. |
| `truncated` | type="boolean" — Whether k omitted known candidates; false does not imply semantic completeness or availability of built structure artifacts. |

<a id="local_mcp-book_guide"></a>

### book_guide

Open, refine, or close an ephemeral visitor guide session. Use when: Maintain a visitor-only ephemeral guided-reading cursor. Do not read Resident reader or memory state.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/5`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": true,
  "idempotentHint": false,
  "openWorldHint": true,
  "readOnlyHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["guide","close"]; default="guide" | guide opens or refines an ephemeral visitor session and requires intent; close ends it and requires session_id. Defaults to guide. |
| `anchor_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID in this book to anchor guided reading. Omit or null when no location is chosen; unused for close. |
| `feedback` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional reader feedback to combine with intent when generating the next guide; unused for close. |
| `intent` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Nonblank natural-language reading goal, required on every guide action, including session refinement; unused for close. |
| `session_id` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Session identifier returned by an earlier guide call. For guide, omit or null to open a session, or supply it to refine that session; required for close. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
[
  {
    "properties": {
      "action": {
        "enum": [
          "guide"
        ]
      },
      "intent": {
        "minLength": 1,
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "required": [
      "intent"
    ]
  },
  {
    "properties": {
      "action": {
        "enum": [
          "close"
        ]
      },
      "session_id": {
        "minLength": 1,
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "required": [
      "action",
      "session_id"
    ]
  }
]
```

<a id="local_mcp-book_paper_lexicon"></a>

### book_paper_lexicon

Return the current paper's lexicon projection for aligning term, abbreviation, and dataset candidates. It returns explicit unavailable status when the sidecar is absent. Use when: Align paper terms, abbreviations, and dataset candidates. Do not treat candidates as verified definitions without source text.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/8`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | 无具名参数 | 见完整 schema |

<a id="local_mcp-book_paper_reading_guide"></a>

### book_paper_reading_guide

Return the read-only PaperReadingGuide projection, combining paper metadata and lexicon, BookStructure, graph, discourse, and source text into ten paper questions, a codebook, and abstract-reading aids. It never creates or changes persistent truth. Use when: Read a deterministic paper-reading projection. Do not use it to mutate persistent truth.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

| `stage` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["passive","active","critical","creative"]; default="passive" | Reading stage: passive, active, critical or creative. Defaults to passive. |
