<a id="local_mcp-book_query"></a>

### book_query

Answer a self-contained semantic question about explicit referents by resolving targets first and then reading source evidence around the frozen bindings. Use when: Answer definition, explanation, relation, or comparison obligations for explicit referents. Do not use for literal occurrence location.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/11`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": false,
  "openWorldHint": true,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `anchor_lid` | 是 | type="string" | Required known LID in this book anchoring referent resolution. It provides local context without restricting evidence retrieval to this node. |
| `intent` | 是 | type="string"; enum=["definition","explanation","relation","comparison"] | Question kind: definition or explanation requires 1..3 targets; relation or comparison requires 2..3 separately named targets. |
| `obligations` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=3 | One to three answer requirements, each in its own requirement object; these guide evidence sufficiency for the requested answer. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string" | Self-contained, nonblank semantic question about the named targets, with enough context to resolve referents. Use literal search for exact wording or first/previous occurrences. |
| `targets` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1; maxItems=3 | Explicit referents to resolve before reading evidence. Each item is nonblank and unique after trimming and lowercasing; name comparison/relation targets separately, not as one combined phrase. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

**输入分支约束**（嵌套对象及互斥规则见完整 schema）

```json
[
  {
    "properties": {
      "intent": {
        "enum": [
          "definition",
          "explanation"
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  {
    "properties": {
      "intent": {
        "enum": [
          "relation",
          "comparison"
        ]
      },
      "targets": {
        "minItems": 2
      }
    }
  }
]
```

<a id="local_mcp-book_query-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "anchor_lid": "1.1",
    "intent": "comparison",
    "obligations": [
      {
        "requirement": "State the activation condition at each location."
      },
      {
        "requirement": "Compare whether equality activates each threshold, citing both passages."
      }
    ],
    "query": "How do the activation conditions of the alpha threshold and beta threshold differ?",
    "targets": [
      "alpha threshold",
      "beta threshold"
    ]
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `answer` | type=["string","null"] |
| `bindings` | type="array" |
| `citations` | type="array" |
| `model_supplement` | type="array" |
| `status` | type="string"; enum=["complete"] / type="string"; enum=["partial"] / type="string"; enum=["insufficient"] / type="string"; enum=["invalid_plan"] / type="string"; enum=["ambiguous"] / type="string"; enum=["unresolved"] |
| `support` | type="array" |
| `missing_requirements` | type="array" |
| `target_issues` | type="array" |
| `candidates` | type="array" |
| `target` | type="string" |

<a id="local_mcp-book_search"></a>

### book_search

Find relevant passages in the current material using lightweight lexical relevance ranking over canonical source. No graph, embeddings or semantic prebuild is required. Results are bounded candidates, not evidence or exhaustive semantic matches. Use when: Locate passages for a natural-language question when the source location is unknown; optionally restrict to a known subtree. Read each chosen passage with its returned book_text call before explaining or citing. For an already selected passage read it directly. For first, previous or every exact occurrence use book_search_text.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/12`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `focus` | 按分支／可选 | anyOf: type="string"; enum=["definition","procedure","explanation","example"] / type="null" | Optional kind of original passage to locate using the book's concept and discourse index. |
| `limit` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; default=8; minimum=1; maximum=20 | Maximum ranked candidate passages, 1..20; omit or null for 8. Scores rank candidates and are not confidence. A bounded result cannot establish semantic exhaustiveness. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | Nonblank natural-language question or terms to find relevant canonical-source passages. Ranking is lexical; translated or synonymous terms can help when the original wording differs. |
| `within_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID restricting candidates to this node and its descendants. Omit or null to search the current material. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_search-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "limit": 4,
    "query": "threshold activation conditions",
    "within_lid": "1"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `hits` | type="array" |
| `query` | type="string" |
| `reading` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" |
| `total_candidates` | type="integer"; minimum=0 — Number of lexically matching windows in the selected scope, not a claim that all passages with related meaning have been found. |
| `version` | type="string" |

<a id="local_mcp-book_search_text"></a>

### book_search_text

Enumerate the complete set of literal-text occurrences in canonical source, returning stable source UTF-16 ranges, the total count, and a pagination cursor. Use when: Locate the first, previous, or every lexical occurrence before interpreting it. Do not use lexical exhaustiveness as proof of semantic exhaustiveness.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/13`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cursor` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Opaque next_cursor from the preceding search result. Omit or null for the first page; preserve query, match_mode, scope, order and page_size on continuation. A changed source revision invalidates it. |
| `match_mode` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["exact","normalized"]; default="exact" | exact (default) matches unchanged text; normalized applies NFKC, full non-Turkic case folding and whitespace collapsing. Both locate literal occurrences, not semantic equivalents; result ranges use original-source UTF-16 positions. |
| `order` | 按分支／可选 | type="string"; enum=["document","reverse_document"]; default="document" | document (default) returns earliest occurrences first; reverse_document returns latest first. Uses canonical source order, not lexical LID sorting. Combine before with reverse_document for previous occurrences. |
| `page_size` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; default=20; minimum=1; maximum=50 | Maximum literal occurrences per search page, 1..50, default 20. Does not change total_occurrences or exhaustive coverage of the requested literal scope; preserve it when using cursor. |
| `query` | 是 | type="string"; minLength=1; maxLength=4096 | Literal text to locate in canonical source, containing non-whitespace text and at most 4,096 Unicode scalar values. Exact mode preserves query whitespace; this is not a semantic question or regex. Read chosen hits using their start_lid/end_lid. |
| `scope` | 按分支／可选 | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" | Optional source scope using known LIDs from this book. Omit, null or {} searches the whole book; within_lid and relative_to intersect, and each whole hit must fit the resulting range. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_search_text-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "match_mode": "exact",
    "order": "document",
    "page_size": 1,
    "query": "threshold"
  },
  {
    "match_mode": "normalized",
    "order": "reverse_document",
    "page_size": 1,
    "query": "THRESHOLD",
    "scope": {
      "relative_to": {
        "direction": "before",
        "lid": "1.3"
      },
      "within_lid": "1"
    }
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `exhaustive` | type="boolean" |
| `next_cursor` | type=["string","null"] |
| `occurrences` | type="array" |
| `section_counts` | type="array" |
| `source_revision` | type="string" |
| `total_lids` | type="integer"; minimum=0 |
| `total_occurrences` | type="integer"; minimum=0 |
| `version` | type="string" |

<a id="local_mcp-book_structure"></a>

### book_structure

Project BookStructure to explain a LID's role in the whole-book spine, throughline, and key stops. Without at, return the whole-book overview; when the sidecar is absent, return explicit unavailable status. Use when: Inspect the structural role of a LID or the whole-book overview. Do not use as a source-text search surface.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/14`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `at` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Optional known LID in this book to locate its structural role or current route segment. Omit or null for the whole-book view; this is a location, not a natural-language query. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_structure-examples"></a>

```json
    "at": "1.2"
  }
]
```

|---|---|
| `at` | type=["string","null"] |
| `available` | type="boolean" |
| `key_stops` | type="array" |
| `spine_index` | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=0 |
| `spine_unit` | anyOf: type="object"; additionalProperties=false / type="null" |
| `throughlines` | type="array" |

<a id="local_mcp-book_synthesize"></a>

### book_synthesize

Synthesize a caller-supplied discrete LID set without expanding retrieval, returning an answer whose citations are a subset of the input LIDs. Use when: Synthesize a caller-supplied, frozen set of LIDs. Do not expand evidence beyond the supplied LIDs.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/15`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": false,
  "openWorldHint": true,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lids` | 是 | type="array"; minItems=1 | Nonempty list of known LIDs selected as the complete evidence scope. Synthesis reads only these nodes and returns citations drawn from this list, without expanding retrieval. |
| `task` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Synthesis instruction for the supplied LIDs; omit or null to synthesize their content without an additional task. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_synthesize-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "lids": [
      "1.1",
      "1.2"
    ],
    "task": "Summarize these two threshold conditions using only these passages; state any evidence gaps."
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `answer` | type=["string","null"] |
| `batched` | type="boolean" |
| `citations` | type="array" |
| `evidence_chain` | type="array" |
| `model_supplement` | type="array" |
| `related_concepts` | type="array" |
| `source_lids` | type="array" |
| `suggested_probing` | type="array" |

<a id="local_mcp-book_text"></a>

### book_text

Read canonical source text by one LID or an inclusive LID range. Optional math_context annotates the delivered passage and supplies next_calls for missing statement evidence and referenced results; annotations and calls do not observe unread text. Use when: Read source text after a LID has been located. Do not use for lexical discovery when no LID is known.

**来源符号**：[crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs:tools_list_result](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp.rs)；[crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs:input_schema](../crates/book-tool-contracts/src/lib.rs)；[crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs:local](../crates/reader-service/src/mcp_output_schema.rs)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/local_mcp/16`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false,
  "readOnlyHint": true
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `end_lid` | 按分支／可选 | type=["string","null"] | Inclusive final LID in source reading order; for a search hit, use end_lid. Reads from lid's span start through end_lid's span end. Omit or null to read only lid's span. |
| `lid` | 是 | type="string" | Known LID from this book's locating or structure results; for a search hit, use start_lid. Reads the node's canonical source span, including descendants. |
| `start_utf16` | 按分支／可选 | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=0 | Absolute canonical-source UTF-16 position copied from a text continuation. Keep the original lid/end_lid. Omit or null for the initial read; a continuation returns a bounded page and its source_range, requested_range and next_start_utf16. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="local_mcp-book_text-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "end_lid": "1.2",
    "lid": "1.1"
  }
]
```

**结果与覆盖**

以下字段取自实际输出 schema；完整分支、嵌套类型及数组元素约束见本项完整契约。

| 字段路径 | 类型／约束／语义 |
|---|---|
| `lid` | type="string" |
| `math_context` | type="array" |
| `text` | type="string" |
| `next_start_utf16` | type=["integer","null"]; minimum=0 |
| `requested_range` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false — 内容区间(半开 [start, end)),映射回源文。 切片0 = **UTF-16 code unit 下标**(TS segment 用 JS 串下标);字节精确化留后 `[ADR-0024]`。 ⇒ Rust book.text 须按 UTF-16 语义切原文(encode_utf16 索引),勿按 UTF-8 字节直切(中文会错位)。 |
| `source_range` | type="object"; additionalProperties=false — 内容区间(半开 [start, end)),映射回源文。 切片0 = **UTF-16 code unit 下标**(TS segment 用 JS 串下标);字节精确化留后 `[ADR-0024]`。 ⇒ Rust book.text 须按 UTF-16 语义切原文(encode_utf16 索引),勿按 UTF-8 字节直切(中文会错位)。 |
<a id="executor"></a>

## 专用 Build Executor

| 工具 | 输入示例数 | 输出 schema |
|---|---:|---|
| [executor.generation.start](#executor-executor-generation-start) | 1 | 未发布 |
| [executor.input.next](#executor-executor-input-next) | 2 | 未发布 |
| [executor.open](#executor-executor-open) | 1 | 未发布 |
| [executor.submit_candidate](#executor-executor-submit_candidate) | 1 | 未发布 |

<a id="executor-executor-generation-start"></a>

### executor.generation.start

Only the dedicated `understand_book_executor` Executor may call this tool. After consuming all input, confirm the final batch and create or replay its semantic attempt. Only GENERATE grants candidate generation: use its output_contract, opaque_session_ref and candidate_sink_ref. The generation session can differ from the delivery session. Lost responses replay the same active attempt; stale_generation_session requires Driver recovery, not reuse of an old sink. Follow WAIT/DONE without generating. Examples use test-session refs; replace refs and ordinals with the exact values from your current handoff or tool results.

**来源符号**：[packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts:BUILD_EXECUTOR_TOOL_CONTRACTS_V3](../packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts)；[skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts:createBuildExecutorMcpSession](../skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/executor/0`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false,
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | 是 | const="automatic_build_executor_generation_start_request.v3" | — |
| `opaque_session_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=75; pattern="^absession1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Delivery session ref whose complete input you consumed. |
| `generation_input_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=73; pattern="^abinput1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Input ref paired with that delivery session. |
| `confirmed_through_ordinal` | 是 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | last_ordinal of the consumed batch with final_for_generation=true; this confirms the final batch and requests the generation grant. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

```json
    "generation_input_ref": "abinput1_1d48529092b7116aa9592bc13ee866267e442acd662e7873576cb6c84e975859",
    "confirmed_through_ordinal": 1
  }
]
```

<a id="executor-executor-input-next"></a>

### executor.input.next

Only the dedicated `understand_book_executor` Executor may call this tool. Read the next ordered INPUT_BATCH. Start with DELIVER_INPUT.next_request, consume every chunk in ordinal order, then acknowledge a non-final batch by its last_ordinal to request the next batch. Repeating the current request replays its batch. Delivery and acknowledgement update the session ledger. When final_for_generation=true, confirm the final last_ordinal with executor.generation.start instead of input.next. Examples show first read and non-final acknowledgement (the DSH fixture delivers one chunk per batch). Examples use test-session refs; replace refs and ordinals with the exact values from your current handoff or tool results.

**来源符号**：[packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts:BUILD_EXECUTOR_TOOL_CONTRACTS_V3](../packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts)；[skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts:createBuildExecutorMcpSession](../skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/executor/1`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false,
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | 是 | const="automatic_build_executor_input_next_request.v4" | — |
| `opaque_session_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=75; pattern="^absession1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Delivery session ref from the current DELIVER_INPUT.next_request. |
| `generation_input_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=73; pattern="^abinput1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Input ref from that same delivery; keep it paired with its session. |
| `ack_through_ordinal` | 按分支／可选 | type="integer"; minimum=0 | Last ordinal actually consumed in the preceding non-final batch. Omit for first read; on resume copy next_request exactly. Never acknowledge the final batch here. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="executor-executor-open"></a>

### executor.open

Only the dedicated `understand_book_executor` Executor may call this tool. Open or resume the assigned handoff. DELIVER_INPUT provides an input_manifest and a ready-to-call next_request; WAIT provides retry_after_ms; DONE is the code-owned terminal status. Reopen the same handoff on a replacement connection to resume unconfirmed input or rehydrate an active generation. A stale_generation_session/lease_expired requires Driver recovery and a new handoff on a fresh connection. Examples use test-session refs; replace refs and ordinals with the exact values from your current handoff or tool results.

**来源符号**：[packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts:BUILD_EXECUTOR_TOOL_CONTRACTS_V3](../packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts)；[skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts:createBuildExecutorMcpSession](../skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/executor/2`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false,
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | 是 | const="automatic_build_executor_open_request.v3" | — |
| `opaque_handoff_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=75; pattern="^abhandoff1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Copy the complete assigned opaque handoff ref; its record version does not change this request version. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="executor-executor-submit_candidate"></a>

### executor.submit_candidate

Only the dedicated `understand_book_executor` Executor may call this tool. Submit one JSON value matching the current GENERATE.output_contract, within its candidate and serialized-request budgets. Copy both refs from that GENERATE, including after recovery. Code writes the private sink, validates and returns the next action or DONE status without echoing the candidate. Candidate submission alone is not proof of acceptance. candidate_sink_unavailable permits replay of the identical candidate on the same active attempt; lease_expired requires Driver recovery. The empty graph example is a valid synthetic Pass1 candidate, not a universal output shape. Examples use test-session refs; replace refs and ordinals with the exact values from your current handoff or tool results.

**来源符号**：[packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts:BUILD_EXECUTOR_TOOL_CONTRACTS_V3](../packages/core/src/build-executor-tool-contract.ts)；[skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts:createBuildExecutorMcpSession](../skills/build/build-executor-mcp.ts)。

**完整契约**：[tool-contracts.json](tool-contracts.json)，JSON Pointer：`/executor/3`。

**发布注解**

```json
{
  "readOnlyHint": false,
  "destructiveHint": false,
  "idempotentHint": true,
  "openWorldHint": false
}
```

**输入参数**

| 字段 | 必填 | 类型与约束 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | 是 | const="automatic_build_executor_candidate_submit.v3" | — |
| `opaque_session_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=75; pattern="^absession1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Generation session ref from the current GENERATE, not DELIVER_INPUT. |
| `candidate_sink_ref` | 是 | type="string"; maxLength=72; pattern="^absink1_[a-f0-9]{64}$" | Private sink ref paired with that current generation session; a replacement generation supplies a new pair. |
| `candidate` | 是 | oneOf: type="null" / type="boolean" / type="number" / type="string" / type="array" / type="object"; additionalProperties=true | Structured JSON value satisfying the delivered semantic prompt and output_contract. Pass the value directly, not a filename or a JSON-encoded string. |

顶层输入拒绝未声明字段。

<a id="tutor-active"></a>

### active

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/active`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

<a id="tutor-active_response"></a>

### active_response

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "learner_context": {
    "pending_assessment_refs": [
      "action:actual-open"
    ],
    "uninterpreted_fact_refs": [
      "usage:actual-user"
    ]
  },
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/active_response`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

In the earlier 10 km journey, 10 minutes moving plus 5 stopped gives T = 15 minutes = 0.25 hours, so the whole-trip average is 10/0.25 = 40 km/h. The stop belongs to that elapsed interval even though it adds no distance.",
```json
      "intent_origin": "current_request",
      "intent_quote": "This time, derive the formula directly using the previous journey.",
      "interaction_intent": "direct_explanation",
      "kind": "explain",
      "move_id": "derive-speed-on-request",

        "choice": "conclude",

      },
      "prompt": "Use the earlier journey to derive the whole-trip average-speed formula at the requested depth, even if an earlier preference favored simple examples.",

        "capability": "explanation",
        "expected_performance": "Connect the stopped interval to the elapsed time of this same whole journey.",
        "learning_focus": "Average speed",
        "object_refs": [],

            "start_lid": "1.1"
          }
```

### choice_compare

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "current_action": {
    "accepted_request": "UI request",
    "action": {
      "move_kind": "compare",
      "origin": "ui_choice",
      "teaching_choice": {
        "delivery_ref": "delivery:fixture",
        "option_id": "next"
      }
    },
    "answer_ref": null,
    "answer_text": null,
    "expected_chat_head": null,
    "presentation_follow_up": null,
    "source_bindings": [],
    "source_excerpt": null,
    "target": null,
    "user_supplement": ""
  },
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/choice_compare`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

<a id="tutor-choice_observe"></a>

### choice_observe

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "current_action": {
    "accepted_request": "UI request",
    "action": {
      "move_kind": "observe",
      "origin": "ui_choice",
      "teaching_choice": {
        "delivery_ref": "delivery:fixture",
        "option_id": "next"
      }
    },
    "answer_ref": null,
    "answer_text": null,
    "expected_chat_head": null,
    "presentation_follow_up": null,
    "source_bindings": [],
    "source_excerpt": null,
    "target": null,
    "user_supplement": ""
  },
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/choice_observe`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

<a id="tutor-clarify_answer"></a>

### clarify_answer

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "current_action": {
    "accepted_request": "UI request",
    "action": {
      "entry_action": "clarify_answer",
      "origin": "ui_entry"
    },
    "answer_ref": null,
    "answer_text": null,
    "expected_chat_head": null,
    "presentation_follow_up": null,
    "source_bindings": [],
    "source_excerpt": null,
    "target": null,
    "user_supplement": ""
  },
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/clarify_answer`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

完整投影：`/tutor_states/confirmed_preference`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

### explain_selection

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "current_action": {
    "accepted_request": "UI request",
    "action": {
      "entry_action": "explain_selection",
      "origin": "ui_entry"
    },
    "answer_ref": null,
    "answer_text": null,
    "expected_chat_head": null,
    "presentation_follow_up": null,
    "source_bindings": [],
    "source_excerpt": null,
    "target": null,
    "user_supplement": ""
  },
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/explain_selection`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

```json
      "delivery_text": "Define the average speed as the constant speed that would cover the same total distance d during the same elapsed time T. A constant speed v covers vT, so matching this journey gives vT = d. With T positive, dividing both sides by T gives v = d/T. In the earlier 10 km journey, 10 minutes moving plus 5 stopped gives T = 15 minutes = 0.25 hours, so the whole-trip average is 10/0.25 = 40 km/h. Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.
```

<a id="tutor-observing"></a>

### observing

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "status": "observing"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/observing`。

Answer the ordinary request. The background observation job records the learner's expression, prior help and knowledge links after delivery. Do not call evidence to duplicate that automatic record. trace rereads delivered facts; understanding reads saved interpretations. Only when the learner explicitly asks to save or correct a saved understanding, first read it with understanding, then use evidence with nature=hypothesis for the requested source-grounded update. Correcting your explanation is an ordinary dialogue reply, not a request to replace a saved interpretation. A UI click or assistant answer is not learner understanding evidence. Examples use an average-speed fixture; replace references and content with actual learner facts and source text. Examples are not evidence. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

<a id="tutor-observing-examples"></a>

**发布示例**

```json
[
  {
    "after": 0,
    "operation": "trace"
  },
  {
    "operation": "understanding"
  }
]
```

Correcting your explanation is an ordinary dialogue reply, not a request to replace a saved interpretation. A UI click or assistant answer is not learner understanding evidence. Examples use an average-speed fixture; replace references and content with actual learner facts and source text. Examples are not evidence. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. <a id="tutor-supplement"></a>

### supplement

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "current_action": {
    "accepted_request": "UI request",
    "action": {
      "entry_action": "explain_selection",
      "origin": "ui_entry"
    },
    "answer_ref": null,
    "answer_text": null,
    "expected_chat_head": null,
    "presentation_follow_up": null,
    "source_bindings": [],
    "source_excerpt": null,
    "target": null,
    "user_supplement": "Let me try first."
  },
  "status": "active"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/supplement`。

Select one source-grounded move after reading original text, video transcript/frames/audio review, or prepared music with music.read this turn. Video and music targets use current-turn source.present IDs in source_ref_ids and source_bindings:[]; the host binds exact source provenance. Supply target with learning_focus, expected_performance, capability, exact source_bindings for full text LIDs (start_lid, end_lid; omit source_id/source_revision to use the runtime-bound current source), and optional object_refs. object_ids may be empty when no applicable formal objects exist. If using available objects, read them with material and include their exact revisions in target.object_refs; missing cognitive material does not block teaching. trace rereads private delivered facts. Explicit direct explanation overrides guided inquiry. Select with operation=select and a move; final delivery presents it. Optional presentation must already be delivered in this turn. Optional assessment freezes private criteria before delivery. assess evaluates an existing formal response in one isolated model call; understanding reads evidence; evidence proposes a sourced ungraded interpretation. Page calculations never assess correctness. Private keys and help must not enter public text. No direct mastery updates. For evidence, understanding separates demonstrated capabilities, concrete difficulties, and unconfirmed parts. Questions alone are not misconceptions; supplied explanations or copied notes do not prove independent use. Use note_links with exact offered NoteRef and a quote from that saved version only when the interpretation concerns that part. known_focuses offers stable private relationships: reuse focus_ref and its exact learning_focus only for the same relationship; new relationships omit it. For open assessment, each rubric item should carry target (same TeachingTarget shape, at most one object, its specific capability) and optional note_refs drawn from move.note_refs. Split concept understanding from calculation criteria. Without this attribution only an aggregate task result is retained. Never give every object the whole-question result. For select, teaching fields belong inside move; operation, finish and delivery_scope are root fields. For a retained draft, use revise: edits changes paragraphs, move_changes changes participation/response_expectation/coach_adjustment, and edits:[] retains all text. The host reconstructs and validates the complete select. Resubmit a full select only when no draft_ref exists or source/design/other fields must change; preserve its full delivery_text. Examples use an average-speed fixture, not learner facts: replace content and references with actual material read this turn. Applicable assess/hypothesis examples appear in the current learner view. Angle-bracket references are placeholders, never event IDs. For assess, take the original response ID from pending_assessment_refs in the current learner view or trace. For a hypothesis, choose actual relevant learner-expression IDs from uninterpreted_fact_refs or trace, and read their original words before interpreting them.

```json
    "operation": "understanding"
  },
  {
    "action_ref": "action:to3-discrimination",
    "interpretation": "The response distinguishes a source-supported quantity from an unsupported property.",
    "object_id": "speed",
    "operation": "evidence",
    "operation_id": "to3-discrimination-evidence",
    "response_quote": "The source supports total distance",
    "sources": [
      {
        "lid": "1.1",
        "quote": "total distance"
      }
    ]
  }
]
```

<a id="tutor-unready"></a>

### unready

**状态输入**

```json
{
  "status": "unready"
}
```

完整投影：`/tutor_states/unready`。

该状态不暴露 tutor.step。

## 已知限制

- 表面目录代表当前源码定义，实际运行还受权限、材料能力、状态与工具暴露计划约束。新状态分支应扩展原 Tutor 契约场景，生成器继续消费这些场景。
- 未发布示例、annotations 或 outputSchema 的条目已明确标注。它们的类型化扩展按实际消费需求推进。
- reader.note 返回 EXISTING 时已有笔记仍有效，但现有 Goal reader_action 完成判断可能缺少创建 effect；见 [TO5 已知限制](https://github.com/adaelon/understand-book/blob/625b4405af3b011e87d4eb256f819c8075f88613/docs/performance/tool-usability/to5/README.md#已知限制)。
- 本文验证契约与生成一致性；真实 Agent 选用、完成率和成本收益由 TO14 验收。
